(file state is current in your context — no need to Read it back)"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"ed135b88-1ee7-4ebc-b785-ba2b7d707846","uuid":"5ef89f11-2c68-4d26-92a5-598658e39a1e","timestamp":"2026-07-28T12:36:16.069Z","tool_use_result":{"filePath":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/security-goal-002/2026-07-28T122808Z-claude-with-skill-1/workspace/src/LendingPool.sol","oldString":"        uint256 debt = debtOf(borrower);\n        if (debt == 0) revert NoDebt();\n\n        uint256 collateralPrice = oracle.collateralPriceUsd();\n        uint256 debtPrice = oracle.debtPriceUsd();\n        uint256 collateral = collateralOf[borrower];\n        uint256 collateralValue = _collateralValueUsd(collateral, collateralPrice);\n        uint256 debtValue = _debtValueUsd(debt, debtPrice);\n\n        if (debtValue * BPS <= collateralValue * LIQUIDATION_THRESHOLD_BPS) {\n            revert PositionHealthy(_healthFactor(collateralValue, debtValue));\n        }\n\n        uint256 maxRepay = debt.mulDiv(MAX_CLOSE_FACTOR_BPS, BPS, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n        if (debt - maxRepay < minDebt) maxRepay = debt;\n        if (repayAmount > maxRepay) revert RepayExceedsCloseFactor(repayAmount, maxRepay);\n\n        repaid = repayAmount;\n        seized = _collateralForRepay(repaid, collateralPrice, debtPrice);\n        if (seized > collateral) {\n            seized = collateral;\n            repaid = _repayForCollateral(seized, collateralPrice, debtPrice);\n            if (repaid > debt) repaid = debt;\n        }\n    }","newString":"        uint256 debt = debtOf(borrower);\n        if (debt == 0) revert NoDebt();\n        return _computeLiquidation(borrower, debt, repayAmount);\n    }","originalFile":"// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity 0.8.28;\n\nimport {IERC20} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/IERC20.sol\";\nimport {IERC20Metadata} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/extensions/IERC20Metadata.sol\";\nimport {SafeERC20} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/utils/SafeERC20.sol\";\nimport {Ownable} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/access/Ownable.sol\";\nimport {Ownable2Step} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/access/Ownable2Step.sol\";\nimport {Pausable} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/Pausable.sol\";\nimport {ReentrancyGuard} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/ReentrancyGuard.sol\";\nimport {Math} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/math/Math.sol\";\n\nimport {IPriceOracle} from \"./interfaces/IPriceOracle.sol\";\n\n/// @title LendingPool\n/// @notice Single-market overcollateralised borrowing: lock WETH, borrow USDC.\n///\n/// @dev Design notes that matter for review:\n///\n///      * Token decimals are read from the tokens at construction. Nothing in here\n///        assumes 18 decimals — USDC has 6, and every conversion goes through the\n///        cached `_collateralUnit` / `_debtUnit` scalars.\n///      * All value comparisons happen in 1e18-scaled USD, using an oracle price for\n///        *both* legs. The debt asset is not assumed to be worth $1.\n///      * Debt is stored as index shares. One global `borrowIndex` accrues interest\n///        for everyone, so no per-user loop is ever needed.\n///      * Rounding always favours the pool: debt owed rounds up, collateral seized\n///        and assets paid out round down.\n///      * External calls happen after state writes (CEI) and every entry point that\n///        moves tokens is `nonReentrant`.\ncontract LendingPool is Ownable2Step, Pausable, ReentrancyGuard {\n    using SafeERC20 for IERC20;\n    using Math for uint256;\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Errors\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    error ZeroAddress();\n    error ZeroAmount();\n    error InvalidDecimals();\n    error RateTooHigh(uint256 rateBps);\n    error MinDebtTooHigh(uint256 minDebt);\n    error InsufficientCollateral();\n    error InsufficientLiquidity();\n    error InsufficientShares();\n    error PositionUnhealthy(uint256 healthFactorWad);\n    error PositionHealthy(uint256 healthFactorWad);\n    error DebtBelowMinimum(uint256 debt, uint256 minDebt);\n    error RepayExceedsCloseFactor(uint256 requested, uint256 maxAllowed);\n    error NoDebt();\n    error NothingSeized();\n    error SlippageProtection(uint256 actual, uint256 bound);\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Constants\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    uint256 public constant WAD = 1e18;\n    uint256 public constant BPS = 10_000;\n\n    /// @notice Max debt value as a share of collateral value when *opening* exposure.\n    uint256 public constant MAX_LTV_BPS = 7_000; // 70%\n\n    /// @notice Debt value above this share of collateral value is liquidatable.\n    uint256 public constant LIQUIDATION_THRESHOLD_BPS = 8_500; // 85%\n\n    /// @notice Extra collateral a liquidator receives on top of what they repaid.\n    uint256 public constant LIQUIDATION_BONUS_BPS = 500; // 5%\n\n    /// @notice Max fraction of a position's debt that can be closed in one call.\n    /// @dev A partial close is enough to restore health; forcing full closure would\n    ///      hand liquidators more collateral (and more bonus) than the position's\n    ///      risk justifies.\n    uint256 public constant MAX_CLOSE_FACTOR_BPS = 5_000; // 50%\n\n    uint256 public constant SECONDS_PER_YEAR = 365 days;\n\n    /// @notice Ceiling on the flat borrow rate the owner can configure.\n    uint256 public constant MAX_INTEREST_RATE_BPS = 5_000; // 50% APR\n\n    /// @dev Virtual shares/assets offset. Makes the classic first-depositor share\n    ///      inflation attack uneconomical: an attacker would have to donate ~1e6x\n    ///      the victim's deposit to round their shares to zero.\n    uint256 private constant VIRTUAL_SHARES = 1e6;\n    uint256 private constant VIRTUAL_ASSETS = 1;\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Immutables\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    /// @notice Collateral asset (WETH on mainnet).\n    IERC20 public immutable collateralToken;\n\n    /// @notice Borrowable asset (USDC on mainnet).\n    IERC20 public immutable debtToken;\n\n    /// @dev 10 ** decimals() of each token, read at deploy time. Never hardcoded.\n    uint256 private immutable _collateralUnit;\n    uint256 private immutable _debtUnit;\n\n    /// @notice Hard ceiling on `minDebt`, so the owner cannot price everyone out.\n    uint256 public immutable maxMinDebt;\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Storage\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    IPriceOracle public oracle;\n\n    /// @notice Flat annual borrow rate, in basis points.\n    uint256 public interestRateBps;\n\n    /// @notice Smallest debt a position may carry (in debt-token units), so that a\n    ///         position is always worth more to liquidate than it costs in gas.\n    uint256 public minDebt;\n\n    /// @notice Cumulative interest index, 1e18-scaled. Starts at 1e18, only grows.\n    uint256 public borrowIndex;\n\n    uint256 public lastAccrualTimestamp;\n\n    uint256 public totalDebtShares;\n    uint256 public totalSupplyShares;\n\n    mapping(address account => uint256 amount) public collateralOf;\n    mapping(address account => uint256 shares) public debtSharesOf;\n    mapping(address account => uint256 shares) public supplySharesOf;\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Events\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    event Supplied(address indexed lender, address indexed onBehalfOf, uint256 assets, uint256 shares);\n    event Withdrawn(address indexed lender, address indexed to, uint256 assets, uint256 shares);\n    event CollateralDeposited(address indexed caller, address indexed onBehalfOf, uint256 amount);\n    event CollateralWithdrawn(address indexed borrower, address indexed to, uint256 amount);\n    event Borrowed(address indexed borrower, address indexed to, uint256 amount, uint256 shares);\n    event Repaid(address indexed caller, address indexed onBehalfOf, uint256 amount, uint256 shares);\n    event Liquidated(\n        address indexed liquidator,\n        address indexed borrower,\n        uint256 repaidAmount,\n        uint256 seizedCollateral,\n        uint256 debtSharesBurned\n    );\n    event InterestAccrued(uint256 borrowIndex, uint256 interestAccrued);\n    event OracleUpdated(address indexed previousOracle, address indexed newOracle);\n    event InterestRateUpdated(uint256 previousRateBps, uint256 newRateBps);\n    event MinDebtUpdated(uint256 previousMinDebt, uint256 newMinDebt);\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Construction\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    constructor(\n        address collateralToken_,\n        address debtToken_,\n        address oracle_,\n        address owner_,\n        uint256 interestRateBps_,\n        uint256 minDebt_\n    ) Ownable(owner_) {\n        if (collateralToken_ == address(0) || debtToken_ == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n        if (collateralToken_ == debtToken_) revert ZeroAddress();\n        if (oracle_ == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n        // Ownable(owner_) already rejects the zero owner.\n\n        collateralToken = IERC20(collateralToken_);\n        debtToken = IERC20(debtToken_);\n\n        uint8 collateralDecimals = IERC20Metadata(collateralToken_).decimals();\n        uint8 debtDecimals = IERC20Metadata(debtToken_).decimals();\n        if (collateralDecimals > 36 || debtDecimals > 36) revert InvalidDecimals();\n        _collateralUnit = 10 ** collateralDecimals;\n        _debtUnit = 10 ** debtDecimals;\n\n        // 10k whole debt-tokens is a generous upper bound for a dust floor.\n        maxMinDebt = 10_000 * _debtUnit;\n\n        oracle = IPriceOracle(oracle_);\n        emit OracleUpdated(address(0), oracle_);\n\n        if (interestRateBps_ > MAX_INTEREST_RATE_BPS) revert RateTooHigh(interestRateBps_);\n        interestRateBps = interestRateBps_;\n        emit InterestRateUpdated(0, interestRateBps_);\n\n        if (minDebt_ > maxMinDebt) revert MinDebtTooHigh(minDebt_);\n        minDebt = minDebt_;\n        emit MinDebtUpdated(0, minDebt_);\n\n        borrowIndex = WAD;\n        lastAccrualTimestamp = block.timestamp;\n    }\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Interest accrual\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    /// @notice Bring the borrow index up to date. Safe to call by anyone at any time.\n    function accrueInterest() public {\n        uint256 elapsed = block.timestamp - lastAccrualTimestamp;\n        if (elapsed == 0) return;\n        lastAccrualTimestamp = block.timestamp;\n\n        uint256 shares = totalDebtShares;\n        if (shares == 0) return; // No borrowers: nothing to charge, and no retroactive catch-up.\n\n        uint256 index = borrowIndex;\n        // Simple interest over the elapsed window; compounds every time this runs.\n        // Multiply before dividing so the rate keeps its precision.\n        uint256 growth = index.mulDiv(interestRateBps * elapsed, BPS * SECONDS_PER_YEAR);\n        if (growth == 0) return;\n\n        uint256 debtBefore = shares.mulDiv(index, WAD);\n        borrowIndex = index + growth;\n        uint256 debtAfter = shares.mulDiv(index + growth, WAD);\n\n        emit InterestAccrued(index + growth, debtAfter - debtBefore);\n    }\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Lender side\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    /// @notice Supply `assets` of the debt token and receive supply shares.\n    /// @param assets Amount of debt token to supply.\n    /// @param onBehalfOf Account credited with the shares.\n    /// @param minSharesOut Slippage bound; revert if fewer shares would be minted.\n    /// @return shares Shares minted.\n    function supply(uint256 assets, address onBehalfOf, uint256 minSharesOut)\n        external\n        nonReentrant\n        whenNotPaused\n        returns (uint256 shares)\n    {\n        if (assets == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        if (onBehalfOf == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n\n        accrueInterest();\n\n        // Snapshot before pulling tokens: the incoming assets must not price their\n        // own shares.\n        uint256 assetsBefore = _totalAssets();\n        uint256 received = _pull(debtToken, msg.sender, assets);\n\n        shares = received.mulDiv(totalSupplyShares + VIRTUAL_SHARES, assetsBefore + VIRTUAL_ASSETS, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n        if (shares == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        if (shares < minSharesOut) revert SlippageProtection(shares, minSharesOut);\n\n        totalSupplyShares += shares;\n        supplySharesOf[onBehalfOf] += shares;\n\n        emit Supplied(msg.sender, onBehalfOf, received, shares);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Burn supply shares and withdraw the underlying debt token.\n    /// @param shares Supply shares to burn.\n    /// @param to Recipient of the assets.\n    /// @param minAssetsOut Slippage bound; revert if fewer assets would be returned.\n    /// @return assets Assets transferred out.\n    /// @dev Deliberately NOT pausable. Freezing lender exits is a rug vector; the\n    ///      only thing that can block a withdrawal here is genuinely absent liquidity.\n    function withdraw(uint256 shares, address to, uint256 minAssetsOut)\n        external\n        nonReentrant\n        returns (uint256 assets)\n    {\n        if (shares == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        if (to == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n\n        accrueInterest();\n\n        uint256 balance = supplySharesOf[msg.sender];\n        if (shares > balance) revert InsufficientShares();\n\n        assets = shares.mulDiv(_totalAssets() + VIRTUAL_ASSETS, totalSupplyShares + VIRTUAL_SHARES, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n        if (assets == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        if (assets < minAssetsOut) revert SlippageProtection(assets, minAssetsOut);\n        if (assets > _cash()) revert InsufficientLiquidity();\n\n        // Effects before the transfer.\n        supplySharesOf[msg.sender] = balance - shares;\n        totalSupplyShares -= shares;\n\n        debtToken.safeTransfer(to, assets);\n\n        emit Withdrawn(msg.sender, to, assets, shares);\n    }\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Borrower side\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    /// @notice Lock collateral for `onBehalfOf`.\n    /// @dev Anyone may add collateral to any position — that can only help the\n    ///      position's health, so it needs no authorisation.\n    function depositCollateral(uint256 amount, address onBehalfOf) external nonReentrant {\n        if (amount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        if (onBehalfOf == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n\n        uint256 received = _pull(collateralToken, msg.sender, amount);\n        collateralOf[onBehalfOf] += received;\n\n        emit CollateralDeposited(msg.sender, onBehalfOf, received);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Withdraw collateral, provided the position stays within max LTV.\n    /// @dev Not pausable, and skips the oracle entirely for debt-free positions, so\n    ///      an unencumbered depositor can always exit even if pricing is down.\n    function withdrawCollateral(uint256 amount, address to) external nonReentrant {\n        if (amount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        if (to == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n\n        accrueInterest();\n\n        uint256 balance = collateralOf[msg.sender];\n        if (amount > balance) revert InsufficientCollateral();\n\n        collateralOf[msg.sender] = balance - amount;\n        _requireWithinMaxLtv(msg.sender);\n\n        collateralToken.safeTransfer(to, amount);\n\n        emit CollateralWithdrawn(msg.sender, to, amount);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Borrow the debt token against previously deposited collateral.\n    function borrow(uint256 amount, address to) external nonReentrant whenNotPaused returns (uint256 shares) {\n        if (amount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        if (to == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n\n        accrueInterest();\n\n        if (amount > _cash()) revert InsufficientLiquidity();\n\n        // Round up: the borrower must never owe less than they took.\n        shares = amount.mulDiv(WAD, borrowIndex, Math.Rounding.Ceil);\n        if (shares == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n\n        totalDebtShares += shares;\n        debtSharesOf[msg.sender] += shares;\n\n        uint256 debt = _debtOf(msg.sender);\n        if (debt < minDebt) revert DebtBelowMinimum(debt, minDebt);\n\n        _requireWithinMaxLtv(msg.sender);\n\n        debtToken.safeTransfer(to, amount);\n\n        emit Borrowed(msg.sender, to, amount, shares);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Repay debt for `onBehalfOf`. Pass `type(uint256).max` to clear it fully.\n    /// @dev Not pausable: a borrower must always be able to get out of debt.\n    /// @return repaid Amount of debt token actually pulled from the caller.\n    function repay(uint256 amount, address onBehalfOf) external nonReentrant returns (uint256 repaid) {\n        if (amount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        if (onBehalfOf == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n\n        accrueInterest();\n\n        uint256 debt = _debtOf(onBehalfOf);\n        if (debt == 0) revert NoDebt();\n\n        repaid = amount > debt ? debt : amount;\n\n        uint256 sharesBurned;\n        if (repaid == debt) {\n            sharesBurned = debtSharesOf[onBehalfOf];\n        } else {\n            // Round down: burn no more debt than was actually paid for.\n            sharesBurned = repaid.mulDiv(WAD, borrowIndex, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n            if (sharesBurned == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        }\n\n        debtSharesOf[onBehalfOf] -= sharesBurned;\n        totalDebtShares -= sharesBurned;\n\n        uint256 remaining = _debtOf(onBehalfOf);\n        if (remaining != 0 && remaining < minDebt) revert DebtBelowMinimum(remaining, minDebt);\n\n        uint256 received = _pull(debtToken, msg.sender, repaid);\n        // A fee-on-transfer debt token would break the accounting above; refuse it.\n        if (received != repaid) revert SlippageProtection(received, repaid);\n\n        emit Repaid(msg.sender, onBehalfOf, repaid, sharesBurned);\n    }\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Liquidation\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    /// @notice Repay part of an unhealthy position's debt and seize its collateral\n    ///         at a `LIQUIDATION_BONUS_BPS` discount.\n    /// @param borrower Position to liquidate.\n    /// @param repayAmount Debt token the caller is willing to repay. Capped at the\n    ///        close factor, and further capped by the collateral actually available.\n    /// @param minCollateralOut Slippage bound; revert if less collateral is seized.\n    ///        Set this — the price can move between simulation and inclusion.\n    /// @return repaid Debt token actually pulled from the liquidator.\n    /// @return seized Collateral transferred to the liquidator.\n    /// @dev Not pausable. Liquidation is the mechanism that keeps the pool solvent;\n    ///      it must not be switchable off.\n    function liquidate(address borrower, uint256 repayAmount, address to, uint256 minCollateralOut)\n        external\n        nonReentrant\n        returns (uint256 repaid, uint256 seized)\n    {\n        if (repayAmount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        if (borrower == address(0) || to == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n\n        accrueInterest();\n\n        uint256 debt = _debtOf(borrower);\n        if (debt == 0) revert NoDebt();\n\n        (repaid, seized) = _computeLiquidation(borrower, debt, repayAmount);\n        if (seized < minCollateralOut) revert SlippageProtection(seized, minCollateralOut);\n\n        uint256 sharesBurned;\n        if (repaid == debt) {\n            sharesBurned = debtSharesOf[borrower];\n        } else {\n            sharesBurned = repaid.mulDiv(WAD, borrowIndex, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n            // A repay too small to burn a single share would take collateral\n            // without reducing debt. Reject it.\n            if (sharesBurned == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        }\n\n        // Effects.\n        debtSharesOf[borrower] -= sharesBurned;\n        totalDebtShares -= sharesBurned;\n        collateralOf[borrower] -= seized;\n\n        // Interactions.\n        if (_pull(debtToken, msg.sender, repaid) != repaid) revert SlippageProtection(0, repaid);\n        collateralToken.safeTransfer(to, seized);\n\n        emit Liquidated(msg.sender, borrower, repaid, seized, sharesBurned);\n    }\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Views\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    /// @notice Current debt of `account`, including interest accrued so far.\n    /// @dev View-only: does not write the index. Values reflect `block.timestamp`.\n    function debtOf(address account) public view returns (uint256) {\n        return debtSharesOf[account].mulDiv(_currentBorrowIndex(), WAD, Math.Rounding.Ceil);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Total outstanding borrows including pending interest.\n    function totalDebt() public view returns (uint256) {\n        return totalDebtShares.mulDiv(_currentBorrowIndex(), WAD, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Idle debt token held by the pool.\n    function availableLiquidity() external view returns (uint256) {\n        return _cash();\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Debt token backing all supply shares: idle cash plus outstanding debt.\n    function totalAssets() external view returns (uint256) {\n        return _cash() + totalDebt();\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Assets a lender's shares are currently worth.\n    function convertToAssets(uint256 shares) external view returns (uint256) {\n        return shares.mulDiv(_cash() + totalDebt() + VIRTUAL_ASSETS, totalSupplyShares + VIRTUAL_SHARES, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Shares that supplying `assets` would mint right now.\n    function convertToShares(uint256 assets) external view returns (uint256) {\n        return assets.mulDiv(totalSupplyShares + VIRTUAL_SHARES, _cash() + totalDebt() + VIRTUAL_ASSETS, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Full health snapshot of a position.\n    /// @return collateralValueUsd 1e18-scaled USD value of locked collateral.\n    /// @return debtValueUsd 1e18-scaled USD value of outstanding debt.\n    /// @return healthFactorWad 1e18 == exactly at the liquidation threshold; below\n    ///         1e18 means liquidatable. `type(uint256).max` when there is no debt.\n    /// @return liquidatable Whether {liquidate} would accept this position now.\n    function positionHealth(address account)\n        external\n        view\n        returns (uint256 collateralValueUsd, uint256 debtValueUsd, uint256 healthFactorWad, bool liquidatable)\n    {\n        collateralValueUsd = _collateralValueUsd(collateralOf[account], oracle.collateralPriceUsd());\n        debtValueUsd = _debtValueUsd(debtOf(account), oracle.debtPriceUsd());\n        healthFactorWad = _healthFactor(collateralValueUsd, debtValueUsd);\n        liquidatable = debtValueUsd * BPS > collateralValueUsd * LIQUIDATION_THRESHOLD_BPS;\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Additional debt token `account` could borrow right now, ignoring\n    ///         available liquidity.\n    function maxBorrowable(address account) external view returns (uint256) {\n        uint256 collateralValue = _collateralValueUsd(collateralOf[account], oracle.collateralPriceUsd());\n        uint256 debtPrice = oracle.debtPriceUsd();\n        uint256 debtValueAllowed = collateralValue.mulDiv(MAX_LTV_BPS, BPS, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n        uint256 debtValueNow = _debtValueUsd(debtOf(account), debtPrice);\n        if (debtValueNow >= debtValueAllowed) return 0;\n        return (debtValueAllowed - debtValueNow).mulDiv(_debtUnit, debtPrice, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Simulate a liquidation: what a given repay would seize, after the\n    ///         close-factor and collateral caps are applied.\n    /// @dev Reverts for the same reasons {liquidate} would, so a liquidator bot can\n    ///      use it as a dry run.\n    function previewLiquidation(address borrower, uint256 repayAmount)\n        external\n        view\n        returns (uint256 repaid, uint256 seized)\n    {\n        uint256 debt = debtOf(borrower);\n        if (debt == 0) revert NoDebt();\n\n        uint256 collateralPrice = oracle.collateralPriceUsd();\n        uint256 debtPrice = oracle.debtPriceUsd();\n        uint256 collateral = collateralOf[borrower];\n        uint256 collateralValue = _collateralValueUsd(collateral, collateralPrice);\n        uint256 debtValue = _debtValueUsd(debt, debtPrice);\n\n        if (debtValue * BPS <= collateralValue * LIQUIDATION_THRESHOLD_BPS) {\n            revert PositionHealthy(_healthFactor(collateralValue, debtValue));\n        }\n\n        uint256 maxRepay = debt.mulDiv(MAX_CLOSE_FACTOR_BPS, BPS, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n        if (debt - maxRepay < minDebt) maxRepay = debt;\n        if (repayAmount > maxRepay) revert RepayExceedsCloseFactor(repayAmount, maxRepay);\n\n        repaid = repayAmount;\n        seized = _collateralForRepay(repaid, collateralPrice, debtPrice);\n        if (seized > collateral) {\n            seized = collateral;\n            repaid = _repayForCollateral(seized, collateralPrice, debtPrice);\n            if (repaid > debt) repaid = debt;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Owner controls\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    /// @notice Point the pool at a different price source.\n    /// @dev The single most dangerous privilege here: a malicious oracle can make\n    ///      every position liquidatable. Owner MUST be a timelock behind a multisig.\n    function setOracle(address newOracle) external onlyOwner {\n        if (newOracle == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n        // Sanity-check the new source before committing to it.\n        if (IPriceOracle(newOracle).collateralPriceUsd() == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        if (IPriceOracle(newOracle).debtPriceUsd() == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n\n        emit OracleUpdated(address(oracle), newOracle);\n        oracle = IPriceOracle(newOracle);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Change the flat annual borrow rate.\n    /// @dev Accrues at the old rate first so the change is never retroactive.\n    function setInterestRate(uint256 newRateBps) external onlyOwner {\n        if (newRateBps > MAX_INTEREST_RATE_BPS) revert RateTooHigh(newRateBps);\n        accrueInterest();\n        emit InterestRateUpdated(interestRateBps, newRateBps);\n        interestRateBps = newRateBps;\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Change the dust floor on position size.\n    function setMinDebt(uint256 newMinDebt) external onlyOwner {\n        if (newMinDebt > maxMinDebt) revert MinDebtTooHigh(newMinDebt);\n        emit MinDebtUpdated(minDebt, newMinDebt);\n        minDebt = newMinDebt;\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Halt new supply and new borrows.\n    /// @dev Repay, withdraw, and liquidate stay open while paused by design — the\n    ///      owner can stop the pool growing, but cannot trap anyone's funds.\n    function pause() external onlyOwner {\n        _pause();\n    }\n\n    function unpause() external onlyOwner {\n        _unpause();\n    }\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Internals\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    function _cash() private view returns (uint256) {\n        return debtToken.balanceOf(address(this));\n    }\n\n    function _totalAssets() private view returns (uint256) {\n        return _cash() + totalDebtShares.mulDiv(borrowIndex, WAD, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n    }\n\n    /// @dev Index including interest that has accrued but not yet been written.\n    function _currentBorrowIndex() private view returns (uint256) {\n        uint256 elapsed = block.timestamp - lastAccrualTimestamp;\n        if (elapsed == 0 || totalDebtShares == 0) return borrowIndex;\n        return borrowIndex + borrowIndex.mulDiv(interestRateBps * elapsed, BPS * SECONDS_PER_YEAR);\n    }\n\n    /// @dev Only valid immediately after {accrueInterest}.\n    function _debtOf(address account) private view returns (uint256) {\n        return debtSharesOf[account].mulDiv(borrowIndex, WAD, Math.Rounding.Ceil);\n    }\n\n    function _collateralValueUsd(uint256 amount, uint256 price) private view returns (uint256) {\n        return amount.mulDiv(price, _collateralUnit, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n    }\n\n    function _debtValueUsd(uint256 amount, uint256 price) private view returns (uint256) {\n        return amount.mulDiv(price, _debtUnit, Math.Rounding.Ceil);\n    }\n\n    function _healthFactor(uint256 collateralValue, uint256 debtValue) private pure returns (uint256) {\n        if (debtValue == 0) return type(uint256).max;\n        return collateralValue.mulDiv(LIQUIDATION_THRESHOLD_BPS * WAD, debtValue * BPS, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n    }\n\n    /// @dev Collateral owed for repaying `repayAmount`, including the bonus.\n    ///      Rounds down — the liquidator never receives more than the formula.\n    function _collateralForRepay(uint256 repayAmount, uint256 collateralPrice, uint256 debtPrice)\n        private\n        view\n        returns (uint256)\n    {\n        uint256 repayValue = repayAmount.mulDiv(debtPrice, _debtUnit, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n        uint256 seizeValue = repayValue.mulDiv(BPS + LIQUIDATION_BONUS_BPS, BPS, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n        return seizeValue.mulDiv(_collateralUnit, collateralPrice, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n    }\n\n    /// @dev Inverse of {_collateralForRepay}: the repay that exactly buys `seized`.\n    ///      Rounds down so a clamped liquidator is never overcharged.\n    function _repayForCollateral(uint256 seized, uint256 collateralPrice, uint256 debtPrice)\n        private\n        view\n        returns (uint256)\n    {\n        uint256 seizeValue = seized.mulDiv(collateralPrice, _collateralUnit, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n        uint256 repayValue = seizeValue.mulDiv(BPS, BPS + LIQUIDATION_BONUS_BPS, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n        return repayValue.mulDiv(_debtUnit, debtPrice, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n    }\n\n    /// @dev Reverts unless `account` is at or below {MAX_LTV_BPS}. Debt-free\n    ///      positions short-circuit without touching the oracle.\n    function _requireWithinMaxLtv(address account) private view {\n        uint256 debt = _debtOf(account);\n        if (debt == 0) return;\n\n        uint256 collateralValue = _collateralValueUsd(collateralOf[account], oracle.collateralPriceUsd());\n        uint256 debtValue = _debtValueUsd(debt, oracle.debtPriceUsd());\n\n        if (debtValue * BPS > collateralValue * MAX_LTV_BPS) {\n            revert PositionUnhealthy(_healthFactor(collateralValue, debtValue));\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// @dev Pulls tokens and returns the amount actually received, so a\n    ///      fee-on-transfer token can never credit more than it delivered.\n    function _pull(IERC20 token, address from, uint256 amount) private returns (uint256) {\n        uint256 balanceBefore = token.balanceOf(address(this));\n        token.safeTransferFrom(from, address(this), amount);\n        return token.balanceOf(address(this)) - balanceBefore;\n    }\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":514,"oldLines":28,"newStart":514,"newLines":7,"lines":["     {","         uint256 debt = debtOf(borrower);","         if (debt == 0) revert NoDebt();","-","-        uint256 collateralPrice = oracle.collateralPriceUsd();","-        uint256 debtPrice = oracle.debtPriceUsd();","-        uint256 collateral = collateralOf[borrower];","-        uint256 collateralValue = _collateralValueUsd(collateral, collateralPrice);","-        uint256 debtValue = _debtValueUsd(debt, debtPrice);","-","-        if (debtValue * BPS <= collateralValue * LIQUIDATION_THRESHOLD_BPS) {","-            revert PositionHealthy(_healthFactor(collateralValue, debtValue));","-        }","-","-        uint256 maxRepay = debt.mulDiv(MAX_CLOSE_FACTOR_BPS, BPS, Math.Rounding.Floor);","-        if (debt - maxRepay < minDebt) maxRepay = debt;","-        if (repayAmount > maxRepay) revert RepayExceedsCloseFactor(repayAmount, maxRepay);","-","-        repaid = repayAmount;","-        seized = _collateralForRepay(repaid, collateralPrice, debtPrice);","-        if (seized > collateral) {","-            seized = collateral;","-            repaid = _repayForCollateral(seized, collateralPrice, debtPrice);","-            if (repaid > debt) repaid = debt;","-        }","+        return _computeLiquidation(borrower, debt, repayAmount);","     }"," ","     // ---------------------------------------------------------------------"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}
{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CdUTyaNKXSi5XoCHYFyXy","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01AZtfZSyypcneRf97WWxQ8P","name":"Edit","input":{"replace_all":false,"file_path":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/security-goal-002/2026-07-28T122808Z-claude-with-skill-1/workspace/src/LendingPool.sol","old_string":"    /// @dev Collateral owed for repaying `repayAmount`, including the bonus.","new_string":"    /// @dev Shared by {liquidate} and {previewLiquidation} so the simulation and the\n    ///      real thing can never drift apart. Reverts if the position is not\n    ///      liquidatable or the requested repay breaches the close factor.\n    /// @param debt Borrower debt at the caller's chosen index (written or pending).\n    function _computeLiquidation(address borrower, uint256 debt, uint256 repayAmount)\n        private\n        view\n        returns (uint256 repaid, uint256 seized)\n    {\n        uint256 collateralPrice = oracle.collateralPriceUsd();\n        uint256 debtPrice = oracle.debtPriceUsd();\n\n        uint256 collateral = collateralOf[borrower];\n        uint256 collateralValue = _collateralValueUsd(collateral, collateralPrice);\n        uint256 debtValue = _debtValueUsd(debt, debtPrice);\n\n        // Liquidatable iff debt value exceeds the liquidation threshold.\n        if (debtValue * BPS <= collateralValue * LIQUIDATION_THRESHOLD_BPS) {\n            revert PositionHealthy(_healthFactor(collateralValue, debtValue));\n        }\n\n        uint256 maxRepay = debt.mulDiv(MAX_CLOSE_FACTOR_BPS, BPS, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n        // Never leave a dust remainder nobody will bother to liquidate.\n        if (debt - maxRepay < minDebt) maxRepay = debt;\n        if (repayAmount > maxRepay) revert RepayExceedsCloseFactor(repayAmount, maxRepay);\n\n        repaid = repayAmount;\n        seized = _collateralForRepay(repaid, collateralPrice, debtPrice);\n\n        if (seized > collateral) {\n            // Underwater beyond the bonus: the liquidator takes all remaining\n            // collateral and only pays what that collateral is worth net of the\n            // bonus. The shortfall stays with the pool as bad debt rather than\n            // being silently charged to the liquidator.\n            seized = collateral;\n            repaid = _repayForCollateral(seized, collateralPrice, debtPrice);\n            if (repaid > debt) repaid = debt;\n        }\n\n        if (seized == 0 || repaid == 0) revert NothingSeized();\n    }\n\n    /// @dev Collateral owed for repaying `repayAmount`, including the bonus."},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":893,"cache_read_input_tokens":79267,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":893},"output_tokens":61,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"ed135b88-1ee7-4ebc-b785-ba2b7d707846","uuid":"aca5cb18-ca4c-4291-b526-e9f6bb7e290a","timestamp":"2026-07-28T12:36:27.818Z","request_id":"req_011CdUTyX3tgTxmn8NDMbZLE"}

(file state is current in your context — no need to Read it back)"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"ed135b88-1ee7-4ebc-b785-ba2b7d707846","uuid":"db2c273a-8dc8-48a8-bcec-414a7081b9ef","timestamp":"2026-07-28T12:36:27.853Z","tool_use_result":{"filePath":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/security-goal-002/2026-07-28T122808Z-claude-with-skill-1/workspace/src/LendingPool.sol","oldString":"    /// @dev Collateral owed for repaying `repayAmount`, including the bonus.","newString":"    /// @dev Shared by {liquidate} and {previewLiquidation} so the simulation and the\n    ///      real thing can never drift apart. Reverts if the position is not\n    ///      liquidatable or the requested repay breaches the close factor.\n    /// @param debt Borrower debt at the caller's chosen index (written or pending).\n    function _computeLiquidation(address borrower, uint256 debt, uint256 repayAmount)\n        private\n        view\n        returns (uint256 repaid, uint256 seized)\n    {\n        uint256 collateralPrice = oracle.collateralPriceUsd();\n        uint256 debtPrice = oracle.debtPriceUsd();\n\n        uint256 collateral = collateralOf[borrower];\n        uint256 collateralValue = _collateralValueUsd(collateral, collateralPrice);\n        uint256 debtValue = _debtValueUsd(debt, debtPrice);\n\n        // Liquidatable iff debt value exceeds the liquidation threshold.\n        if (debtValue * BPS <= collateralValue * LIQUIDATION_THRESHOLD_BPS) {\n            revert PositionHealthy(_healthFactor(collateralValue, debtValue));\n        }\n\n        uint256 maxRepay = debt.mulDiv(MAX_CLOSE_FACTOR_BPS, BPS, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n        // Never leave a dust remainder nobody will bother to liquidate.\n        if (debt - maxRepay < minDebt) maxRepay = debt;\n        if (repayAmount > maxRepay) revert RepayExceedsCloseFactor(repayAmount, maxRepay);\n\n        repaid = repayAmount;\n        seized = _collateralForRepay(repaid, collateralPrice, debtPrice);\n\n        if (seized > collateral) {\n            // Underwater beyond the bonus: the liquidator takes all remaining\n            // collateral and only pays what that collateral is worth net of the\n            // bonus. The shortfall stays with the pool as bad debt rather than\n            // being silently charged to the liquidator.\n            seized = collateral;\n            repaid = _repayForCollateral(seized, collateralPrice, debtPrice);\n            if (repaid > debt) repaid = debt;\n        }\n\n        if (seized == 0 || repaid == 0) revert NothingSeized();\n    }\n\n    /// @dev Collateral owed for repaying `repayAmount`, including the bonus.","originalFile":"// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity 0.8.28;\n\nimport {IERC20} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/IERC20.sol\";\nimport {IERC20Metadata} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/extensions/IERC20Metadata.sol\";\nimport {SafeERC20} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/utils/SafeERC20.sol\";\nimport {Ownable} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/access/Ownable.sol\";\nimport {Ownable2Step} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/access/Ownable2Step.sol\";\nimport {Pausable} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/Pausable.sol\";\nimport {ReentrancyGuard} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/ReentrancyGuard.sol\";\nimport {Math} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/math/Math.sol\";\n\nimport {IPriceOracle} from \"./interfaces/IPriceOracle.sol\";\n\n/// @title LendingPool\n/// @notice Single-market overcollateralised borrowing: lock WETH, borrow USDC.\n///\n/// @dev Design notes that matter for review:\n///\n///      * Token decimals are read from the tokens at construction. Nothing in here\n///        assumes 18 decimals — USDC has 6, and every conversion goes through the\n///        cached `_collateralUnit` / `_debtUnit` scalars.\n///      * All value comparisons happen in 1e18-scaled USD, using an oracle price for\n///        *both* legs. The debt asset is not assumed to be worth $1.\n///      * Debt is stored as index shares. One global `borrowIndex` accrues interest\n///        for everyone, so no per-user loop is ever needed.\n///      * Rounding always favours the pool: debt owed rounds up, collateral seized\n///        and assets paid out round down.\n///      * External calls happen after state writes (CEI) and every entry point that\n///        moves tokens is `nonReentrant`.\ncontract LendingPool is Ownable2Step, Pausable, ReentrancyGuard {\n    using SafeERC20 for IERC20;\n    using Math for uint256;\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Errors\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    error ZeroAddress();\n    error ZeroAmount();\n    error InvalidDecimals();\n    error RateTooHigh(uint256 rateBps);\n    error MinDebtTooHigh(uint256 minDebt);\n    error InsufficientCollateral();\n    error InsufficientLiquidity();\n    error InsufficientShares();\n    error PositionUnhealthy(uint256 healthFactorWad);\n    error PositionHealthy(uint256 healthFactorWad);\n    error DebtBelowMinimum(uint256 debt, uint256 minDebt);\n    error RepayExceedsCloseFactor(uint256 requested, uint256 maxAllowed);\n    error NoDebt();\n    error NothingSeized();\n    error SlippageProtection(uint256 actual, uint256 bound);\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Constants\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    uint256 public constant WAD = 1e18;\n    uint256 public constant BPS = 10_000;\n\n    /// @notice Max debt value as a share of collateral value when *opening* exposure.\n    uint256 public constant MAX_LTV_BPS = 7_000; // 70%\n\n    /// @notice Debt value above this share of collateral value is liquidatable.\n    uint256 public constant LIQUIDATION_THRESHOLD_BPS = 8_500; // 85%\n\n    /// @notice Extra collateral a liquidator receives on top of what they repaid.\n    uint256 public constant LIQUIDATION_BONUS_BPS = 500; // 5%\n\n    /// @notice Max fraction of a position's debt that can be closed in one call.\n    /// @dev A partial close is enough to restore health; forcing full closure would\n    ///      hand liquidators more collateral (and more bonus) than the position's\n    ///      risk justifies.\n    uint256 public constant MAX_CLOSE_FACTOR_BPS = 5_000; // 50%\n\n    uint256 public constant SECONDS_PER_YEAR = 365 days;\n\n    /// @notice Ceiling on the flat borrow rate the owner can configure.\n    uint256 public constant MAX_INTEREST_RATE_BPS = 5_000; // 50% APR\n\n    /// @dev Virtual shares/assets offset. Makes the classic first-depositor share\n    ///      inflation attack uneconomical: an attacker would have to donate ~1e6x\n    ///      the victim's deposit to round their shares to zero.\n    uint256 private constant VIRTUAL_SHARES = 1e6;\n    uint256 private constant VIRTUAL_ASSETS = 1;\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Immutables\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    /// @notice Collateral asset (WETH on mainnet).\n    IERC20 public immutable collateralToken;\n\n    /// @notice Borrowable asset (USDC on mainnet).\n    IERC20 public immutable debtToken;\n\n    /// @dev 10 ** decimals() of each token, read at deploy time. Never hardcoded.\n    uint256 private immutable _collateralUnit;\n    uint256 private immutable _debtUnit;\n\n    /// @notice Hard ceiling on `minDebt`, so the owner cannot price everyone out.\n    uint256 public immutable maxMinDebt;\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Storage\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    IPriceOracle public oracle;\n\n    /// @notice Flat annual borrow rate, in basis points.\n    uint256 public interestRateBps;\n\n    /// @notice Smallest debt a position may carry (in debt-token units), so that a\n    ///         position is always worth more to liquidate than it costs in gas.\n    uint256 public minDebt;\n\n    /// @notice Cumulative interest index, 1e18-scaled. Starts at 1e18, only grows.\n    uint256 public borrowIndex;\n\n    uint256 public lastAccrualTimestamp;\n\n    uint256 public totalDebtShares;\n    uint256 public totalSupplyShares;\n\n    mapping(address account => uint256 amount) public collateralOf;\n    mapping(address account => uint256 shares) public debtSharesOf;\n    mapping(address account => uint256 shares) public supplySharesOf;\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Events\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    event Supplied(address indexed lender, address indexed onBehalfOf, uint256 assets, uint256 shares);\n    event Withdrawn(address indexed lender, address indexed to, uint256 assets, uint256 shares);\n    event CollateralDeposited(address indexed caller, address indexed onBehalfOf, uint256 amount);\n    event CollateralWithdrawn(address indexed borrower, address indexed to, uint256 amount);\n    event Borrowed(address indexed borrower, address indexed to, uint256 amount, uint256 shares);\n    event Repaid(address indexed caller, address indexed onBehalfOf, uint256 amount, uint256 shares);\n    event Liquidated(\n        address indexed liquidator,\n        address indexed borrower,\n        uint256 repaidAmount,\n        uint256 seizedCollateral,\n        uint256 debtSharesBurned\n    );\n    event InterestAccrued(uint256 borrowIndex, uint256 interestAccrued);\n    event OracleUpdated(address indexed previousOracle, address indexed newOracle);\n    event InterestRateUpdated(uint256 previousRateBps, uint256 newRateBps);\n    event MinDebtUpdated(uint256 previousMinDebt, uint256 newMinDebt);\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Construction\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    constructor(\n        address collateralToken_,\n        address debtToken_,\n        address oracle_,\n        address owner_,\n        uint256 interestRateBps_,\n        uint256 minDebt_\n    ) Ownable(owner_) {\n        if (collateralToken_ == address(0) || debtToken_ == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n        if (collateralToken_ == debtToken_) revert ZeroAddress();\n        if (oracle_ == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n        // Ownable(owner_) already rejects the zero owner.\n\n        collateralToken = IERC20(collateralToken_);\n        debtToken = IERC20(debtToken_);\n\n        uint8 collateralDecimals = IERC20Metadata(collateralToken_).decimals();\n        uint8 debtDecimals = IERC20Metadata(debtToken_).decimals();\n        if (collateralDecimals > 36 || debtDecimals > 36) revert InvalidDecimals();\n        _collateralUnit = 10 ** collateralDecimals;\n        _debtUnit = 10 ** debtDecimals;\n\n        // 10k whole debt-tokens is a generous upper bound for a dust floor.\n        maxMinDebt = 10_000 * _debtUnit;\n\n        oracle = IPriceOracle(oracle_);\n        emit OracleUpdated(address(0), oracle_);\n\n        if (interestRateBps_ > MAX_INTEREST_RATE_BPS) revert RateTooHigh(interestRateBps_);\n        interestRateBps = interestRateBps_;\n        emit InterestRateUpdated(0, interestRateBps_);\n\n        if (minDebt_ > maxMinDebt) revert MinDebtTooHigh(minDebt_);\n        minDebt = minDebt_;\n        emit MinDebtUpdated(0, minDebt_);\n\n        borrowIndex = WAD;\n        lastAccrualTimestamp = block.timestamp;\n    }\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Interest accrual\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    /// @notice Bring the borrow index up to date. Safe to call by anyone at any time.\n    function accrueInterest() public {\n        uint256 elapsed = block.timestamp - lastAccrualTimestamp;\n        if (elapsed == 0) return;\n        lastAccrualTimestamp = block.timestamp;\n\n        uint256 shares = totalDebtShares;\n        if (shares == 0) return; // No borrowers: nothing to charge, and no retroactive catch-up.\n\n        uint256 index = borrowIndex;\n        // Simple interest over the elapsed window; compounds every time this runs.\n        // Multiply before dividing so the rate keeps its precision.\n        uint256 growth = index.mulDiv(interestRateBps * elapsed, BPS * SECONDS_PER_YEAR);\n        if (growth == 0) return;\n\n        uint256 debtBefore = shares.mulDiv(index, WAD);\n        borrowIndex = index + growth;\n        uint256 debtAfter = shares.mulDiv(index + growth, WAD);\n\n        emit InterestAccrued(index + growth, debtAfter - debtBefore);\n    }\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Lender side\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    /// @notice Supply `assets` of the debt token and receive supply shares.\n    /// @param assets Amount of debt token to supply.\n    /// @param onBehalfOf Account credited with the shares.\n    /// @param minSharesOut Slippage bound; revert if fewer shares would be minted.\n    /// @return shares Shares minted.\n    function supply(uint256 assets, address onBehalfOf, uint256 minSharesOut)\n        external\n        nonReentrant\n        whenNotPaused\n        returns (uint256 shares)\n    {\n        if (assets == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        if (onBehalfOf == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n\n        accrueInterest();\n\n        // Snapshot before pulling tokens: the incoming assets must not price their\n        // own shares.\n        uint256 assetsBefore = _totalAssets();\n        uint256 received = _pull(debtToken, msg.sender, assets);\n\n        shares = received.mulDiv(totalSupplyShares + VIRTUAL_SHARES, assetsBefore + VIRTUAL_ASSETS, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n        if (shares == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        if (shares < minSharesOut) revert SlippageProtection(shares, minSharesOut);\n\n        totalSupplyShares += shares;\n        supplySharesOf[onBehalfOf] += shares;\n\n        emit Supplied(msg.sender, onBehalfOf, received, shares);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Burn supply shares and withdraw the underlying debt token.\n    /// @param shares Supply shares to burn.\n    /// @param to Recipient of the assets.\n    /// @param minAssetsOut Slippage bound; revert if fewer assets would be returned.\n    /// @return assets Assets transferred out.\n    /// @dev Deliberately NOT pausable. Freezing lender exits is a rug vector; the\n    ///      only thing that can block a withdrawal here is genuinely absent liquidity.\n    function withdraw(uint256 shares, address to, uint256 minAssetsOut)\n        external\n        nonReentrant\n        returns (uint256 assets)\n    {\n        if (shares == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        if (to == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n\n        accrueInterest();\n\n        uint256 balance = supplySharesOf[msg.sender];\n        if (shares > balance) revert InsufficientShares();\n\n        assets = shares.mulDiv(_totalAssets() + VIRTUAL_ASSETS, totalSupplyShares + VIRTUAL_SHARES, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n        if (assets == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        if (assets < minAssetsOut) revert SlippageProtection(assets, minAssetsOut);\n        if (assets > _cash()) revert InsufficientLiquidity();\n\n        // Effects before the transfer.\n        supplySharesOf[msg.sender] = balance - shares;\n        totalSupplyShares -= shares;\n\n        debtToken.safeTransfer(to, assets);\n\n        emit Withdrawn(msg.sender, to, assets, shares);\n    }\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Borrower side\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    /// @notice Lock collateral for `onBehalfOf`.\n    /// @dev Anyone may add collateral to any position — that can only help the\n    ///      position's health, so it needs no authorisation.\n    function depositCollateral(uint256 amount, address onBehalfOf) external nonReentrant {\n        if (amount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        if (onBehalfOf == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n\n        uint256 received = _pull(collateralToken, msg.sender, amount);\n        collateralOf[onBehalfOf] += received;\n\n        emit CollateralDeposited(msg.sender, onBehalfOf, received);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Withdraw collateral, provided the position stays within max LTV.\n    /// @dev Not pausable, and skips the oracle entirely for debt-free positions, so\n    ///      an unencumbered depositor can always exit even if pricing is down.\n    function withdrawCollateral(uint256 amount, address to) external nonReentrant {\n        if (amount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        if (to == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n\n        accrueInterest();\n\n        uint256 balance = collateralOf[msg.sender];\n        if (amount > balance) revert InsufficientCollateral();\n\n        collateralOf[msg.sender] = balance - amount;\n        _requireWithinMaxLtv(msg.sender);\n\n        collateralToken.safeTransfer(to, amount);\n\n        emit CollateralWithdrawn(msg.sender, to, amount);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Borrow the debt token against previously deposited collateral.\n    function borrow(uint256 amount, address to) external nonReentrant whenNotPaused returns (uint256 shares) {\n        if (amount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        if (to == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n\n        accrueInterest();\n\n        if (amount > _cash()) revert InsufficientLiquidity();\n\n        // Round up: the borrower must never owe less than they took.\n        shares = amount.mulDiv(WAD, borrowIndex, Math.Rounding.Ceil);\n        if (shares == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n\n        totalDebtShares += shares;\n        debtSharesOf[msg.sender] += shares;\n\n        uint256 debt = _debtOf(msg.sender);\n        if (debt < minDebt) revert DebtBelowMinimum(debt, minDebt);\n\n        _requireWithinMaxLtv(msg.sender);\n\n        debtToken.safeTransfer(to, amount);\n\n        emit Borrowed(msg.sender, to, amount, shares);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Repay debt for `onBehalfOf`. Pass `type(uint256).max` to clear it fully.\n    /// @dev Not pausable: a borrower must always be able to get out of debt.\n    /// @return repaid Amount of debt token actually pulled from the caller.\n    function repay(uint256 amount, address onBehalfOf) external nonReentrant returns (uint256 repaid) {\n        if (amount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        if (onBehalfOf == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n\n        accrueInterest();\n\n        uint256 debt = _debtOf(onBehalfOf);\n        if (debt == 0) revert NoDebt();\n\n        repaid = amount > debt ? debt : amount;\n\n        uint256 sharesBurned;\n        if (repaid == debt) {\n            sharesBurned = debtSharesOf[onBehalfOf];\n        } else {\n            // Round down: burn no more debt than was actually paid for.\n            sharesBurned = repaid.mulDiv(WAD, borrowIndex, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n            if (sharesBurned == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        }\n\n        debtSharesOf[onBehalfOf] -= sharesBurned;\n        totalDebtShares -= sharesBurned;\n\n        uint256 remaining = _debtOf(onBehalfOf);\n        if (remaining != 0 && remaining < minDebt) revert DebtBelowMinimum(remaining, minDebt);\n\n        uint256 received = _pull(debtToken, msg.sender, repaid);\n        // A fee-on-transfer debt token would break the accounting above; refuse it.\n        if (received != repaid) revert SlippageProtection(received, repaid);\n\n        emit Repaid(msg.sender, onBehalfOf, repaid, sharesBurned);\n    }\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Liquidation\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    /// @notice Repay part of an unhealthy position's debt and seize its collateral\n    ///         at a `LIQUIDATION_BONUS_BPS` discount.\n    /// @param borrower Position to liquidate.\n    /// @param repayAmount Debt token the caller is willing to repay. Capped at the\n    ///        close factor, and further capped by the collateral actually available.\n    /// @param minCollateralOut Slippage bound; revert if less collateral is seized.\n    ///        Set this — the price can move between simulation and inclusion.\n    /// @return repaid Debt token actually pulled from the liquidator.\n    /// @return seized Collateral transferred to the liquidator.\n    /// @dev Not pausable. Liquidation is the mechanism that keeps the pool solvent;\n    ///      it must not be switchable off.\n    function liquidate(address borrower, uint256 repayAmount, address to, uint256 minCollateralOut)\n        external\n        nonReentrant\n        returns (uint256 repaid, uint256 seized)\n    {\n        if (repayAmount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        if (borrower == address(0) || to == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n\n        accrueInterest();\n\n        uint256 debt = _debtOf(borrower);\n        if (debt == 0) revert NoDebt();\n\n        (repaid, seized) = _computeLiquidation(borrower, debt, repayAmount);\n        if (seized < minCollateralOut) revert SlippageProtection(seized, minCollateralOut);\n\n        uint256 sharesBurned;\n        if (repaid == debt) {\n            sharesBurned = debtSharesOf[borrower];\n        } else {\n            sharesBurned = repaid.mulDiv(WAD, borrowIndex, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n            // A repay too small to burn a single share would take collateral\n            // without reducing debt. Reject it.\n            if (sharesBurned == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        }\n\n        // Effects.\n        debtSharesOf[borrower] -= sharesBurned;\n        totalDebtShares -= sharesBurned;\n        collateralOf[borrower] -= seized;\n\n        // Interactions.\n        if (_pull(debtToken, msg.sender, repaid) != repaid) revert SlippageProtection(0, repaid);\n        collateralToken.safeTransfer(to, seized);\n\n        emit Liquidated(msg.sender, borrower, repaid, seized, sharesBurned);\n    }\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Views\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    /// @notice Current debt of `account`, including interest accrued so far.\n    /// @dev View-only: does not write the index. Values reflect `block.timestamp`.\n    function debtOf(address account) public view returns (uint256) {\n        return debtSharesOf[account].mulDiv(_currentBorrowIndex(), WAD, Math.Rounding.Ceil);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Total outstanding borrows including pending interest.\n    function totalDebt() public view returns (uint256) {\n        return totalDebtShares.mulDiv(_currentBorrowIndex(), WAD, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Idle debt token held by the pool.\n    function availableLiquidity() external view returns (uint256) {\n        return _cash();\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Debt token backing all supply shares: idle cash plus outstanding debt.\n    function totalAssets() external view returns (uint256) {\n        return _cash() + totalDebt();\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Assets a lender's shares are currently worth.\n    function convertToAssets(uint256 shares) external view returns (uint256) {\n        return shares.mulDiv(_cash() + totalDebt() + VIRTUAL_ASSETS, totalSupplyShares + VIRTUAL_SHARES, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Shares that supplying `assets` would mint right now.\n    function convertToShares(uint256 assets) external view returns (uint256) {\n        return assets.mulDiv(totalSupplyShares + VIRTUAL_SHARES, _cash() + totalDebt() + VIRTUAL_ASSETS, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Full health snapshot of a position.\n    /// @return collateralValueUsd 1e18-scaled USD value of locked collateral.\n    /// @return debtValueUsd 1e18-scaled USD value of outstanding debt.\n    /// @return healthFactorWad 1e18 == exactly at the liquidation threshold; below\n    ///         1e18 means liquidatable. `type(uint256).max` when there is no debt.\n    /// @return liquidatable Whether {liquidate} would accept this position now.\n    function positionHealth(address account)\n        external\n        view\n        returns (uint256 collateralValueUsd, uint256 debtValueUsd, uint256 healthFactorWad, bool liquidatable)\n    {\n        collateralValueUsd = _collateralValueUsd(collateralOf[account], oracle.collateralPriceUsd());\n        debtValueUsd = _debtValueUsd(debtOf(account), oracle.debtPriceUsd());\n        healthFactorWad = _healthFactor(collateralValueUsd, debtValueUsd);\n        liquidatable = debtValueUsd * BPS > collateralValueUsd * LIQUIDATION_THRESHOLD_BPS;\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Additional debt token `account` could borrow right now, ignoring\n    ///         available liquidity.\n    function maxBorrowable(address account) external view returns (uint256) {\n        uint256 collateralValue = _collateralValueUsd(collateralOf[account], oracle.collateralPriceUsd());\n        uint256 debtPrice = oracle.debtPriceUsd();\n        uint256 debtValueAllowed = collateralValue.mulDiv(MAX_LTV_BPS, BPS, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n        uint256 debtValueNow = _debtValueUsd(debtOf(account), debtPrice);\n        if (debtValueNow >= debtValueAllowed) return 0;\n        return (debtValueAllowed - debtValueNow).mulDiv(_debtUnit, debtPrice, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Simulate a liquidation: what a given repay would seize, after the\n    ///         close-factor and collateral caps are applied.\n    /// @dev Reverts for the same reasons {liquidate} would, so a liquidator bot can\n    ///      use it as a dry run.\n    function previewLiquidation(address borrower, uint256 repayAmount)\n        external\n        view\n        returns (uint256 repaid, uint256 seized)\n    {\n        uint256 debt = debtOf(borrower);\n        if (debt == 0) revert NoDebt();\n        return _computeLiquidation(borrower, debt, repayAmount);\n    }\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Owner controls\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    /// @notice Point the pool at a different price source.\n    /// @dev The single most dangerous privilege here: a malicious oracle can make\n    ///      every position liquidatable. Owner MUST be a timelock behind a multisig.\n    function setOracle(address newOracle) external onlyOwner {\n        if (newOracle == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n        // Sanity-check the new source before committing to it.\n        if (IPriceOracle(newOracle).collateralPriceUsd() == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        if (IPriceOracle(newOracle).debtPriceUsd() == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n\n        emit OracleUpdated(address(oracle), newOracle);\n        oracle = IPriceOracle(newOracle);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Change the flat annual borrow rate.\n    /// @dev Accrues at the old rate first so the change is never retroactive.\n    function setInterestRate(uint256 newRateBps) external onlyOwner {\n        if (newRateBps > MAX_INTEREST_RATE_BPS) revert RateTooHigh(newRateBps);\n        accrueInterest();\n        emit InterestRateUpdated(interestRateBps, newRateBps);\n        interestRateBps = newRateBps;\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Change the dust floor on position size.\n    function setMinDebt(uint256 newMinDebt) external onlyOwner {\n        if (newMinDebt > maxMinDebt) revert MinDebtTooHigh(newMinDebt);\n        emit MinDebtUpdated(minDebt, newMinDebt);\n        minDebt = newMinDebt;\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Halt new supply and new borrows.\n    /// @dev Repay, withdraw, and liquidate stay open while paused by design — the\n    ///      owner can stop the pool growing, but cannot trap anyone's funds.\n    function pause() external onlyOwner {\n        _pause();\n    }\n\n    function unpause() external onlyOwner {\n        _unpause();\n    }\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Internals\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    function _cash() private view returns (uint256) {\n        return debtToken.balanceOf(address(this));\n    }\n\n    function _totalAssets() private view returns (uint256) {\n        return _cash() + totalDebtShares.mulDiv(borrowIndex, WAD, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n    }\n\n    /// @dev Index including interest that has accrued but not yet been written.\n    function _currentBorrowIndex() private view returns (uint256) {\n        uint256 elapsed = block.timestamp - lastAccrualTimestamp;\n        if (elapsed == 0 || totalDebtShares == 0) return borrowIndex;\n        return borrowIndex + borrowIndex.mulDiv(interestRateBps * elapsed, BPS * SECONDS_PER_YEAR);\n    }\n\n    /// @dev Only valid immediately after {accrueInterest}.\n    function _debtOf(address account) private view returns (uint256) {\n        return debtSharesOf[account].mulDiv(borrowIndex, WAD, Math.Rounding.Ceil);\n    }\n\n    function _collateralValueUsd(uint256 amount, uint256 price) private view returns (uint256) {\n        return amount.mulDiv(price, _collateralUnit, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n    }\n\n    function _debtValueUsd(uint256 amount, uint256 price) private view returns (uint256) {\n        return amount.mulDiv(price, _debtUnit, Math.Rounding.Ceil);\n    }\n\n    function _healthFactor(uint256 collateralValue, uint256 debtValue) private pure returns (uint256) {\n        if (debtValue == 0) return type(uint256).max;\n        return collateralValue.mulDiv(LIQUIDATION_THRESHOLD_BPS * WAD, debtValue * BPS, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n    }\n\n    /// @dev Collateral owed for repaying `repayAmount`, including the bonus.\n    ///      Rounds down — the liquidator never receives more than the formula.\n    function _collateralForRepay(uint256 repayAmount, uint256 collateralPrice, uint256 debtPrice)\n        private\n        view\n        returns (uint256)\n    {\n        uint256 repayValue = repayAmount.mulDiv(debtPrice, _debtUnit, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n        uint256 seizeValue = repayValue.mulDiv(BPS + LIQUIDATION_BONUS_BPS, BPS, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n        return seizeValue.mulDiv(_collateralUnit, collateralPrice, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n    }\n\n    /// @dev Inverse of {_collateralForRepay}: the repay that exactly buys `seized`.\n    ///      Rounds down so a clamped liquidator is never overcharged.\n    function _repayForCollateral(uint256 seized, uint256 collateralPrice, uint256 debtPrice)\n        private\n        view\n        returns (uint256)\n    {\n        uint256 seizeValue = seized.mulDiv(collateralPrice, _collateralUnit, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n        uint256 repayValue = seizeValue.mulDiv(BPS, BPS + LIQUIDATION_BONUS_BPS, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n        return repayValue.mulDiv(_debtUnit, debtPrice, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n    }\n\n    /// @dev Reverts unless `account` is at or below {MAX_LTV_BPS}. Debt-free\n    ///      positions short-circuit without touching the oracle.\n    function _requireWithinMaxLtv(address account) private view {\n        uint256 debt = _debtOf(account);\n        if (debt == 0) return;\n\n        uint256 collateralValue = _collateralValueUsd(collateralOf[account], oracle.collateralPriceUsd());\n        uint256 debtValue = _debtValueUsd(debt, oracle.debtPriceUsd());\n\n        if (debtValue * BPS > collateralValue * MAX_LTV_BPS) {\n            revert PositionUnhealthy(_healthFactor(collateralValue, debtValue));\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// @dev Pulls tokens and returns the amount actually received, so a\n    ///      fee-on-transfer token can never credit more than it delivered.\n    function _pull(IERC20 token, address from, uint256 amount) private returns (uint256) {\n        uint256 balanceBefore = token.balanceOf(address(this));\n        token.safeTransferFrom(from, address(this), amount);\n        return token.balanceOf(address(this)) - balanceBefore;\n    }\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":598,"oldLines":6,"newStart":598,"newLines":48,"lines":["         return collateralValue.mulDiv(LIQUIDATION_THRESHOLD_BPS * WAD, debtValue * BPS, Math.Rounding.Floor);","     }"," ","+    /// @dev Shared by {liquidate} and {previewLiquidation} so the simulation and the","+    ///      real thing can never drift apart. Reverts if the position is not","+    ///      liquidatable or the requested repay breaches the close factor.","+    /// @param debt Borrower debt at the caller's chosen index (written or pending).","+    function _computeLiquidation(address borrower, uint256 debt, uint256 repayAmount)","+        private","+        view","+        returns (uint256 repaid, uint256 seized)","+    {","+        uint256 collateralPrice = oracle.collateralPriceUsd();","+        uint256 debtPrice = oracle.debtPriceUsd();","+","+        uint256 collateral = collateralOf[borrower];","+        uint256 collateralValue = _collateralValueUsd(collateral, collateralPrice);","+        uint256 debtValue = _debtValueUsd(debt, debtPrice);","+","+        // Liquidatable iff debt value exceeds the liquidation threshold.","+        if (debtValue * BPS <= collateralValue * LIQUIDATION_THRESHOLD_BPS) {","+            revert PositionHealthy(_healthFactor(collateralValue, debtValue));","+        }","+","+        uint256 maxRepay = debt.mulDiv(MAX_CLOSE_FACTOR_BPS, BPS, Math.Rounding.Floor);","+        // Never leave a dust remainder nobody will bother to liquidate.","+        if (debt - maxRepay < minDebt) maxRepay = debt;","+        if (repayAmount > maxRepay) revert RepayExceedsCloseFactor(repayAmount, maxRepay);","+","+        repaid = repayAmount;","+        seized = _collateralForRepay(repaid, collateralPrice, debtPrice);","+","+        if (seized > collateral) {","+            // Underwater beyond the bonus: the liquidator takes all remaining","+            // collateral and only pays what that collateral is worth net of the","+            // bonus. The shortfall stays with the pool as bad debt rather than","+            // being silently charged to the liquidator.","+            seized = collateral;","+            repaid = _repayForCollateral(seized, collateralPrice, debtPrice);","+            if (repaid > debt) repaid = debt;","+        }","+","+        if (seized == 0 || repaid == 0) revert NothingSeized();","+    }","+","     /// @dev Collateral owed for repaying `repayAmount`, including the bonus.","     ///      Rounds down — the liquidator never receives more than the formula.","     function _collateralForRepay(uint256 repayAmount, uint256 collateralPrice, uint256 debtPrice)"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}

(file state is current in your context — no need to Read it back)"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"ed135b88-1ee7-4ebc-b785-ba2b7d707846","uuid":"9ea66a67-b79e-4d7c-9495-8890358de10a","timestamp":"2026-07-28T12:36:43.654Z","tool_use_result":{"filePath":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/security-goal-002/2026-07-28T122808Z-claude-with-skill-1/workspace/src/ChainlinkPriceOracle.sol","oldString":"        return uint256(answer) * scale;","newString":"        // casting to 'uint256' is safe because the bounds check above guarantees\n        // answer >= minAnswer > 0 (enforced in the constructor).\n        // forge-lint: disable-next-line(unsafe-typecast)\n        return uint256(answer) * scale;","originalFile":"// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity 0.8.28;\n\nimport {IAggregatorV3} from \"./interfaces/IAggregatorV3.sol\";\nimport {IPriceOracle} from \"./interfaces/IPriceOracle.sol\";\n\n/// @title ChainlinkPriceOracle\n/// @notice Immutable adapter turning two Chainlink USD feeds into the 1e18-scaled\n///         prices {LendingPool} expects.\n/// @dev Fully immutable on purpose: there is no admin that can point a live pool at\n///      a different price mid-flight. If a feed is deprecated or its heartbeat\n///      changes, deploy a new adapter and move the pool onto it (a governed,\n///      observable action) instead of mutating this one.\n///\n///      Both prices are quoted in USD rather than assuming the debt token is worth\n///      exactly $1. A stablecoin depeg is precisely the moment health factors must\n///      stay honest.\ncontract ChainlinkPriceOracle is IPriceOracle {\n    error InvalidAddress();\n    error InvalidBounds();\n    error InvalidStaleness();\n    error StalePrice(address feed, uint256 updatedAt);\n    error PriceOutOfBounds(address feed, int256 answer);\n    error UnsupportedFeedDecimals(address feed, uint8 feedDecimals);\n\n    /// @dev Anything older than a day is not a price, it is a rumour.\n    uint256 private constant MAX_STALENESS_CEILING = 1 days;\n\n    IAggregatorV3 public immutable collateralFeed;\n    IAggregatorV3 public immutable debtFeed;\n\n    /// @notice Max age of a feed answer, in seconds. Should be the feed's heartbeat\n    ///         plus a safety margin.\n    uint256 public immutable collateralMaxStaleness;\n    uint256 public immutable debtMaxStaleness;\n\n    /// @notice Hard sanity band on the raw feed answer, in the feed's own decimals.\n    /// @dev Chainlink aggregators clamp their answer at the underlying aggregator's\n    ///      min/maxAnswer. If the real price ever leaves that band, the feed keeps\n    ///      reporting the boundary value forever and looks perfectly fresh. These\n    ///      bounds turn that silent lie into a revert.\n    int256 public immutable collateralMinAnswer;\n    int256 public immutable collateralMaxAnswer;\n    int256 public immutable debtMinAnswer;\n    int256 public immutable debtMaxAnswer;\n\n    uint256 private immutable collateralScale;\n    uint256 private immutable debtScale;\n\n    struct FeedConfig {\n        IAggregatorV3 feed;\n        uint256 maxStaleness;\n        int256 minAnswer;\n        int256 maxAnswer;\n    }\n\n    constructor(FeedConfig memory collateral, FeedConfig memory debt) {\n        if (address(collateral.feed) == address(0) || address(debt.feed) == address(0)) revert InvalidAddress();\n        if (collateral.maxStaleness == 0 || collateral.maxStaleness > MAX_STALENESS_CEILING) revert InvalidStaleness();\n        if (debt.maxStaleness == 0 || debt.maxStaleness > MAX_STALENESS_CEILING) revert InvalidStaleness();\n        if (collateral.minAnswer <= 0 || collateral.maxAnswer <= collateral.minAnswer) revert InvalidBounds();\n        if (debt.minAnswer <= 0 || debt.maxAnswer <= debt.minAnswer) revert InvalidBounds();\n\n        collateralFeed = collateral.feed;\n        debtFeed = debt.feed;\n        collateralMaxStaleness = collateral.maxStaleness;\n        debtMaxStaleness = debt.maxStaleness;\n        collateralMinAnswer = collateral.minAnswer;\n        collateralMaxAnswer = collateral.maxAnswer;\n        debtMinAnswer = debt.minAnswer;\n        debtMaxAnswer = debt.maxAnswer;\n\n        // Read decimals once: they are fixed for the life of a feed, and caching them\n        // keeps the hot path free of an extra external call.\n        collateralScale = _scaleFor(collateral.feed);\n        debtScale = _scaleFor(debt.feed);\n    }\n\n    /// @inheritdoc IPriceOracle\n    function collateralPriceUsd() external view returns (uint256) {\n        return _price(collateralFeed, collateralMaxStaleness, collateralMinAnswer, collateralMaxAnswer, collateralScale);\n    }\n\n    /// @inheritdoc IPriceOracle\n    function debtPriceUsd() external view returns (uint256) {\n        return _price(debtFeed, debtMaxStaleness, debtMinAnswer, debtMaxAnswer, debtScale);\n    }\n\n    function _price(IAggregatorV3 feed, uint256 maxStaleness, int256 minAnswer, int256 maxAnswer, uint256 scale)\n        private\n        view\n        returns (uint256)\n    {\n        (, int256 answer,, uint256 updatedAt,) = feed.latestRoundData();\n\n        // updatedAt == 0 means the round was never completed. A timestamp in the\n        // future means the feed is misbehaving; treat both as unusable.\n        if (updatedAt == 0 || updatedAt > block.timestamp) revert StalePrice(address(feed), updatedAt);\n        if (block.timestamp - updatedAt > maxStaleness) revert StalePrice(address(feed), updatedAt);\n        if (answer < minAnswer || answer > maxAnswer) revert PriceOutOfBounds(address(feed), answer);\n\n        return uint256(answer) * scale;\n    }\n\n    function _scaleFor(IAggregatorV3 feed) private view returns (uint256) {\n        uint8 feedDecimals = feed.decimals();\n        if (feedDecimals > 18) revert UnsupportedFeedDecimals(address(feed), feedDecimals);\n        return 10 ** (18 - feedDecimals);\n    }\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":99,"oldLines":6,"newStart":99,"newLines":9,"lines":["         if (block.timestamp - updatedAt > maxStaleness) revert StalePrice(address(feed), updatedAt);","         if (answer < minAnswer || answer > maxAnswer) revert PriceOutOfBounds(address(feed), answer);"," ","+        // casting to 'uint256' is safe because the bounds check above guarantees","+        // answer >= minAnswer > 0 (enforced in the constructor).","+        // forge-lint: disable-next-line(unsafe-typecast)","         return uint256(answer) * scale;","     }"," "]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}
