{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CdUU1ruYS42LrXEiteiqe","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_0163dPgnfvY5qkd8MgfA14wn","name":"Bash","input":{"command":"python3 - <<'EOF'\nimport re\np='test/LendingPool.t.sol'\ns=open(p).read()\n\ns = s.replace(\"\"\"        // ETH collapses to $1,000: collateral $10,000 vs $14,000 debt. Even a\n        // half-close cannot be paid for out of the remaining collateral.\n        ethFeed.set(1000e8, block.timestamp);\n\n        vm.prank(keeper);\n        (uint256 repaid, uint256 seized) = pool.liquidate(alice, 7_000e6, keeper, 0);\n\n        // All collateral is taken; the liquidator only pays what it is worth net\n        // of the 5% bonus: $10,000 / 1.05 = 9,523.809523 USDC.\n        assertEq(seized, 10 ether);\n        assertEq(repaid, 9_523_809523);\"\"\",\n\"\"\"        // ETH collapses to $700: collateral $7,000 against $14,000 of debt. Even a\n        // half-close cannot be paid for out of the remaining collateral.\n        ethFeed.set(700e8, block.timestamp);\n\n        vm.prank(keeper);\n        (uint256 repaid, uint256 seized) = pool.liquidate(alice, 7_000e6, keeper, 0);\n\n        // All collateral is taken; the liquidator only pays what it is worth net\n        // of the 5% bonus: $7,000 / 1.05 = 6,666.666666 USDC.\n        assertEq(seized, 10 ether);\n        assertEq(repaid, 6_666_666666);\"\"\")\n\ns = s.replace(\"\"\"        // USDC trades at $1.10: the debt is now worth $15,400 against $20,000.\n        usdcFeed.set(1.1e8, block.timestamp);\"\"\",\n\"\"\"        // USDC trades at $1.25: the debt is now worth $17,500 against $20,000 of\n        // collateral, i.e. 87.5% — past the 85% threshold.\n        usdcFeed.set(1.25e8, block.timestamp);\"\"\")\n\ns = s.replace(\"\"\"        // Pausing does not trap lenders...\n        vm.prank(lender);\n        uint256 assets = pool.withdraw(pool.supplySharesOf(lender) / 10, lender, 0);\n        assertGt(assets, 0);\n\n        // ...but absent liquidity does.\n        vm.prank(lender);\n        vm.expectRevert(LendingPool.InsufficientLiquidity.selector);\n        pool.withdraw(pool.supplySharesOf(lender), lender, 0);\"\"\",\n\"\"\"        // Pausing does not trap lenders...\n        uint256 shares = pool.supplySharesOf(lender);\n        vm.prank(lender);\n        uint256 assets = pool.withdraw(shares / 10, lender, 0);\n        assertGt(assets, 0);\n\n        // ...but absent liquidity does.\n        uint256 remaining = pool.supplySharesOf(lender);\n        vm.prank(lender);\n        vm.expectRevert(LendingPool.InsufficientLiquidity.selector);\n        pool.withdraw(remaining, lender, 0);\"\"\")\n\ns = s.replace(\"collateral = bound(collateral, 0.5 ether, 500 ether);\",\n              \"collateral = bound(collateral, 0.5 ether, 300 ether);\")\nopen(p,'w').write(s)\nEOF\nforge test 2>&1 | grep -v \"^note\\[\" | tail -40"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1566,"cache_read_input_tokens":82735,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1566},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"ed135b88-1ee7-4ebc-b785-ba2b7d707846","uuid":"4c3952b6-b714-45f6-ad22-03c0adfbe249","timestamp":"2026-07-28T12:37:19.704Z","request_id":"req_011CdUU1qautMZQU6EnJ9HQe"}

Adding one gap I spotted while reviewing — bad debt currently stays on the books and inflates lender share price."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":7690,"cache_read_input_tokens":84301,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":7690},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"ed135b88-1ee7-4ebc-b785-ba2b7d707846","uuid":"f39ed6e2-3011-40a9-b58b-417f3299e2ae","timestamp":"2026-07-28T12:37:47.508Z","request_id":"req_011CdUU4Kz1sK6PBdcXhnR1Y"}
{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CdUU4PoxTZX2Fp7h5WyZA","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01StQBPLBTJk2HM2EUZ4sati","name":"Edit","input":{"replace_all":false,"file_path":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/security-goal-002/2026-07-28T122808Z-claude-with-skill-1/workspace/src/LendingPool.sol","old_string":"    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Views\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------","new_string":"    /// @notice Erase the debt of a position that has no collateral left.\n    /// @dev Permissionless and moves no tokens. A position can only reach\n    ///      \"zero collateral, non-zero debt\" by being liquidated while deeply\n    ///      underwater, so the remainder is genuinely unbacked. Recognising it\n    ///      immediately drops the pool's share price for everyone at once. If it\n    ///      lingered, `totalAssets` would keep counting uncollectable debt as an\n    ///      asset and the lenders who withdrew first would be paid out of the\n    ///      lenders who withdrew last.\n    /// @return written Debt token amount written off.\n    function writeOffBadDebt(address borrower) external nonReentrant returns (uint256 written) {\n        accrueInterest();\n\n        if (collateralOf[borrower] != 0) revert PositionHasCollateral();\n\n        uint256 shares = debtSharesOf[borrower];\n        if (shares == 0) revert NoDebt();\n\n        written = _debtOf(borrower);\n        debtSharesOf[borrower] = 0;\n        totalDebtShares -= shares;\n\n        emit BadDebtWrittenOff(borrower, written, shares);\n    }\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Views\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":7690,"cache_read_input_tokens":84301,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":7690},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"ed135b88-1ee7-4ebc-b785-ba2b7d707846","uuid":"7a67fa24-f428-4548-b251-6176013bebff","timestamp":"2026-07-28T12:37:55.221Z","request_id":"req_011CdUU4Kz1sK6PBdcXhnR1Y"}

(file state is current in your context — no need to Read it back)"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"ed135b88-1ee7-4ebc-b785-ba2b7d707846","uuid":"07ba88ee-ed57-4274-9070-9eaae5b908f9","timestamp":"2026-07-28T12:37:55.242Z","tool_use_result":{"filePath":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/security-goal-002/2026-07-28T122808Z-claude-with-skill-1/workspace/src/LendingPool.sol","oldString":"    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Views\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------","newString":"    /// @notice Erase the debt of a position that has no collateral left.\n    /// @dev Permissionless and moves no tokens. A position can only reach\n    ///      \"zero collateral, non-zero debt\" by being liquidated while deeply\n    ///      underwater, so the remainder is genuinely unbacked. Recognising it\n    ///      immediately drops the pool's share price for everyone at once. If it\n    ///      lingered, `totalAssets` would keep counting uncollectable debt as an\n    ///      asset and the lenders who withdrew first would be paid out of the\n    ///      lenders who withdrew last.\n    /// @return written Debt token amount written off.\n    function writeOffBadDebt(address borrower) external nonReentrant returns (uint256 written) {\n        accrueInterest();\n\n        if (collateralOf[borrower] != 0) revert PositionHasCollateral();\n\n        uint256 shares = debtSharesOf[borrower];\n        if (shares == 0) revert NoDebt();\n\n        written = _debtOf(borrower);\n        debtSharesOf[borrower] = 0;\n        totalDebtShares -= shares;\n\n        emit BadDebtWrittenOff(borrower, written, shares);\n    }\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Views\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------","originalFile":"// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity 0.8.28;\n\nimport {IERC20} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/IERC20.sol\";\nimport {IERC20Metadata} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/extensions/IERC20Metadata.sol\";\nimport {SafeERC20} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/utils/SafeERC20.sol\";\nimport {Ownable} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/access/Ownable.sol\";\nimport {Ownable2Step} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/access/Ownable2Step.sol\";\nimport {Pausable} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/Pausable.sol\";\nimport {ReentrancyGuard} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/ReentrancyGuard.sol\";\nimport {Math} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/math/Math.sol\";\n\nimport {IPriceOracle} from \"./interfaces/IPriceOracle.sol\";\n\n/// @title LendingPool\n/// @notice Single-market overcollateralised borrowing: lock WETH, borrow USDC.\n///\n/// @dev Design notes that matter for review:\n///\n///      * Token decimals are read from the tokens at construction. Nothing in here\n///        assumes 18 decimals — USDC has 6, and every conversion goes through the\n///        cached `_collateralUnit` / `_debtUnit` scalars.\n///      * All value comparisons happen in 1e18-scaled USD, using an oracle price for\n///        *both* legs. The debt asset is not assumed to be worth $1.\n///      * Debt is stored as index shares. One global `borrowIndex` accrues interest\n///        for everyone, so no per-user loop is ever needed.\n///      * Rounding always favours the pool: debt owed rounds up, collateral seized\n///        and assets paid out round down.\n///      * External calls happen after state writes (CEI) and every entry point that\n///        moves tokens is `nonReentrant`.\ncontract LendingPool is Ownable2Step, Pausable, ReentrancyGuard {\n    using SafeERC20 for IERC20;\n    using Math for uint256;\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Errors\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    error ZeroAddress();\n    error ZeroAmount();\n    error InvalidDecimals();\n    error RateTooHigh(uint256 rateBps);\n    error MinDebtTooHigh(uint256 minDebt);\n    error InsufficientCollateral();\n    error InsufficientLiquidity();\n    error InsufficientShares();\n    error PositionUnhealthy(uint256 healthFactorWad);\n    error PositionHealthy(uint256 healthFactorWad);\n    error DebtBelowMinimum(uint256 debt, uint256 minDebt);\n    error RepayExceedsCloseFactor(uint256 requested, uint256 maxAllowed);\n    error NoDebt();\n    error NothingSeized();\n    error SlippageProtection(uint256 actual, uint256 bound);\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Constants\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    uint256 public constant WAD = 1e18;\n    uint256 public constant BPS = 10_000;\n\n    /// @notice Max debt value as a share of collateral value when *opening* exposure.\n    uint256 public constant MAX_LTV_BPS = 7_000; // 70%\n\n    /// @notice Debt value above this share of collateral value is liquidatable.\n    uint256 public constant LIQUIDATION_THRESHOLD_BPS = 8_500; // 85%\n\n    /// @notice Extra collateral a liquidator receives on top of what they repaid.\n    uint256 public constant LIQUIDATION_BONUS_BPS = 500; // 5%\n\n    /// @notice Max fraction of a position's debt that can be closed in one call.\n    /// @dev A partial close is enough to restore health; forcing full closure would\n    ///      hand liquidators more collateral (and more bonus) than the position's\n    ///      risk justifies.\n    uint256 public constant MAX_CLOSE_FACTOR_BPS = 5_000; // 50%\n\n    uint256 public constant SECONDS_PER_YEAR = 365 days;\n\n    /// @notice Ceiling on the flat borrow rate the owner can configure.\n    uint256 public constant MAX_INTEREST_RATE_BPS = 5_000; // 50% APR\n\n    /// @dev Virtual shares/assets offset. Makes the classic first-depositor share\n    ///      inflation attack uneconomical: an attacker would have to donate ~1e6x\n    ///      the victim's deposit to round their shares to zero.\n    uint256 private constant VIRTUAL_SHARES = 1e6;\n    uint256 private constant VIRTUAL_ASSETS = 1;\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Immutables\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    /// @notice Collateral asset (WETH on mainnet).\n    IERC20 public immutable collateralToken;\n\n    /// @notice Borrowable asset (USDC on mainnet).\n    IERC20 public immutable debtToken;\n\n    /// @dev 10 ** decimals() of each token, read at deploy time. Never hardcoded.\n    uint256 private immutable _collateralUnit;\n    uint256 private immutable _debtUnit;\n\n    /// @notice Hard ceiling on `minDebt`, so the owner cannot price everyone out.\n    uint256 public immutable maxMinDebt;\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Storage\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    IPriceOracle public oracle;\n\n    /// @notice Flat annual borrow rate, in basis points.\n    uint256 public interestRateBps;\n\n    /// @notice Smallest debt a position may carry (in debt-token units), so that a\n    ///         position is always worth more to liquidate than it costs in gas.\n    uint256 public minDebt;\n\n    /// @notice Cumulative interest index, 1e18-scaled. Starts at 1e18, only grows.\n    uint256 public borrowIndex;\n\n    uint256 public lastAccrualTimestamp;\n\n    uint256 public totalDebtShares;\n    uint256 public totalSupplyShares;\n\n    mapping(address account => uint256 amount) public collateralOf;\n    mapping(address account => uint256 shares) public debtSharesOf;\n    mapping(address account => uint256 shares) public supplySharesOf;\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Events\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    event Supplied(address indexed lender, address indexed onBehalfOf, uint256 assets, uint256 shares);\n    event Withdrawn(address indexed lender, address indexed to, uint256 assets, uint256 shares);\n    event CollateralDeposited(address indexed caller, address indexed onBehalfOf, uint256 amount);\n    event CollateralWithdrawn(address indexed borrower, address indexed to, uint256 amount);\n    event Borrowed(address indexed borrower, address indexed to, uint256 amount, uint256 shares);\n    event Repaid(address indexed caller, address indexed onBehalfOf, uint256 amount, uint256 shares);\n    event Liquidated(\n        address indexed liquidator,\n        address indexed borrower,\n        uint256 repaidAmount,\n        uint256 seizedCollateral,\n        uint256 debtSharesBurned\n    );\n    event InterestAccrued(uint256 borrowIndex, uint256 interestAccrued);\n    event OracleUpdated(address indexed previousOracle, address indexed newOracle);\n    event InterestRateUpdated(uint256 previousRateBps, uint256 newRateBps);\n    event MinDebtUpdated(uint256 previousMinDebt, uint256 newMinDebt);\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Construction\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    constructor(\n        address collateralToken_,\n        address debtToken_,\n        address oracle_,\n        address owner_,\n        uint256 interestRateBps_,\n        uint256 minDebt_\n    ) Ownable(owner_) {\n        if (collateralToken_ == address(0) || debtToken_ == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n        if (collateralToken_ == debtToken_) revert ZeroAddress();\n        if (oracle_ == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n        // Ownable(owner_) already rejects the zero owner.\n\n        collateralToken = IERC20(collateralToken_);\n        debtToken = IERC20(debtToken_);\n\n        uint8 collateralDecimals = IERC20Metadata(collateralToken_).decimals();\n        uint8 debtDecimals = IERC20Metadata(debtToken_).decimals();\n        if (collateralDecimals > 36 || debtDecimals > 36) revert InvalidDecimals();\n        _collateralUnit = 10 ** collateralDecimals;\n        _debtUnit = 10 ** debtDecimals;\n\n        // 10k whole debt-tokens is a generous upper bound for a dust floor.\n        maxMinDebt = 10_000 * _debtUnit;\n\n        oracle = IPriceOracle(oracle_);\n        emit OracleUpdated(address(0), oracle_);\n\n        if (interestRateBps_ > MAX_INTEREST_RATE_BPS) revert RateTooHigh(interestRateBps_);\n        interestRateBps = interestRateBps_;\n        emit InterestRateUpdated(0, interestRateBps_);\n\n        if (minDebt_ > maxMinDebt) revert MinDebtTooHigh(minDebt_);\n        minDebt = minDebt_;\n        emit MinDebtUpdated(0, minDebt_);\n\n        borrowIndex = WAD;\n        lastAccrualTimestamp = block.timestamp;\n    }\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Interest accrual\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    /// @notice Bring the borrow index up to date. Safe to call by anyone at any time.\n    function accrueInterest() public {\n        uint256 elapsed = block.timestamp - lastAccrualTimestamp;\n        if (elapsed == 0) return;\n        lastAccrualTimestamp = block.timestamp;\n\n        uint256 shares = totalDebtShares;\n        if (shares == 0) return; // No borrowers: nothing to charge, and no retroactive catch-up.\n\n        uint256 index = borrowIndex;\n        // Simple interest over the elapsed window; compounds every time this runs.\n        // Multiply before dividing so the rate keeps its precision.\n        uint256 growth = index.mulDiv(interestRateBps * elapsed, BPS * SECONDS_PER_YEAR);\n        if (growth == 0) return;\n\n        uint256 debtBefore = shares.mulDiv(index, WAD);\n        borrowIndex = index + growth;\n        uint256 debtAfter = shares.mulDiv(index + growth, WAD);\n\n        emit InterestAccrued(index + growth, debtAfter - debtBefore);\n    }\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Lender side\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    /// @notice Supply `assets` of the debt token and receive supply shares.\n    /// @param assets Amount of debt token to supply.\n    /// @param onBehalfOf Account credited with the shares.\n    /// @param minSharesOut Slippage bound; revert if fewer shares would be minted.\n    /// @return shares Shares minted.\n    function supply(uint256 assets, address onBehalfOf, uint256 minSharesOut)\n        external\n        nonReentrant\n        whenNotPaused\n        returns (uint256 shares)\n    {\n        if (assets == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        if (onBehalfOf == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n\n        accrueInterest();\n\n        // Snapshot before pulling tokens: the incoming assets must not price their\n        // own shares.\n        uint256 assetsBefore = _totalAssets();\n        uint256 received = _pull(debtToken, msg.sender, assets);\n\n        shares = received.mulDiv(totalSupplyShares + VIRTUAL_SHARES, assetsBefore + VIRTUAL_ASSETS, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n        if (shares == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        if (shares < minSharesOut) revert SlippageProtection(shares, minSharesOut);\n\n        totalSupplyShares += shares;\n        supplySharesOf[onBehalfOf] += shares;\n\n        emit Supplied(msg.sender, onBehalfOf, received, shares);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Burn supply shares and withdraw the underlying debt token.\n    /// @param shares Supply shares to burn.\n    /// @param to Recipient of the assets.\n    /// @param minAssetsOut Slippage bound; revert if fewer assets would be returned.\n    /// @return assets Assets transferred out.\n    /// @dev Deliberately NOT pausable. Freezing lender exits is a rug vector; the\n    ///      only thing that can block a withdrawal here is genuinely absent liquidity.\n    function withdraw(uint256 shares, address to, uint256 minAssetsOut)\n        external\n        nonReentrant\n        returns (uint256 assets)\n    {\n        if (shares == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        if (to == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n\n        accrueInterest();\n\n        uint256 balance = supplySharesOf[msg.sender];\n        if (shares > balance) revert InsufficientShares();\n\n        assets = shares.mulDiv(_totalAssets() + VIRTUAL_ASSETS, totalSupplyShares + VIRTUAL_SHARES, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n        if (assets == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        if (assets < minAssetsOut) revert SlippageProtection(assets, minAssetsOut);\n        if (assets > _cash()) revert InsufficientLiquidity();\n\n        // Effects before the transfer.\n        supplySharesOf[msg.sender] = balance - shares;\n        totalSupplyShares -= shares;\n\n        debtToken.safeTransfer(to, assets);\n\n        emit Withdrawn(msg.sender, to, assets, shares);\n    }\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Borrower side\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    /// @notice Lock collateral for `onBehalfOf`.\n    /// @dev Anyone may add collateral to any position — that can only help the\n    ///      position's health, so it needs no authorisation.\n    function depositCollateral(uint256 amount, address onBehalfOf) external nonReentrant {\n        if (amount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        if (onBehalfOf == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n\n        uint256 received = _pull(collateralToken, msg.sender, amount);\n        collateralOf[onBehalfOf] += received;\n\n        emit CollateralDeposited(msg.sender, onBehalfOf, received);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Withdraw collateral, provided the position stays within max LTV.\n    /// @dev Not pausable, and skips the oracle entirely for debt-free positions, so\n    ///      an unencumbered depositor can always exit even if pricing is down.\n    function withdrawCollateral(uint256 amount, address to) external nonReentrant {\n        if (amount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        if (to == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n\n        accrueInterest();\n\n        uint256 balance = collateralOf[msg.sender];\n        if (amount > balance) revert InsufficientCollateral();\n\n        collateralOf[msg.sender] = balance - amount;\n        _requireWithinMaxLtv(msg.sender);\n\n        collateralToken.safeTransfer(to, amount);\n\n        emit CollateralWithdrawn(msg.sender, to, amount);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Borrow the debt token against previously deposited collateral.\n    function borrow(uint256 amount, address to) external nonReentrant whenNotPaused returns (uint256 shares) {\n        if (amount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        if (to == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n\n        accrueInterest();\n\n        if (amount > _cash()) revert InsufficientLiquidity();\n\n        // Round up: the borrower must never owe less than they took.\n        shares = amount.mulDiv(WAD, borrowIndex, Math.Rounding.Ceil);\n        if (shares == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n\n        totalDebtShares += shares;\n        debtSharesOf[msg.sender] += shares;\n\n        uint256 debt = _debtOf(msg.sender);\n        if (debt < minDebt) revert DebtBelowMinimum(debt, minDebt);\n\n        _requireWithinMaxLtv(msg.sender);\n\n        debtToken.safeTransfer(to, amount);\n\n        emit Borrowed(msg.sender, to, amount, shares);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Repay debt for `onBehalfOf`. Pass `type(uint256).max` to clear it fully.\n    /// @dev Not pausable: a borrower must always be able to get out of debt.\n    /// @return repaid Amount of debt token actually pulled from the caller.\n    function repay(uint256 amount, address onBehalfOf) external nonReentrant returns (uint256 repaid) {\n        if (amount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        if (onBehalfOf == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n\n        accrueInterest();\n\n        uint256 debt = _debtOf(onBehalfOf);\n        if (debt == 0) revert NoDebt();\n\n        repaid = amount > debt ? debt : amount;\n\n        uint256 sharesBurned;\n        if (repaid == debt) {\n            sharesBurned = debtSharesOf[onBehalfOf];\n        } else {\n            // Round down: burn no more debt than was actually paid for.\n            sharesBurned = repaid.mulDiv(WAD, borrowIndex, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n            if (sharesBurned == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        }\n\n        debtSharesOf[onBehalfOf] -= sharesBurned;\n        totalDebtShares -= sharesBurned;\n\n        uint256 remaining = _debtOf(onBehalfOf);\n        if (remaining != 0 && remaining < minDebt) revert DebtBelowMinimum(remaining, minDebt);\n\n        uint256 received = _pull(debtToken, msg.sender, repaid);\n        // A fee-on-transfer debt token would break the accounting above; refuse it.\n        if (received != repaid) revert SlippageProtection(received, repaid);\n\n        emit Repaid(msg.sender, onBehalfOf, repaid, sharesBurned);\n    }\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Liquidation\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    /// @notice Repay part of an unhealthy position's debt and seize its collateral\n    ///         at a `LIQUIDATION_BONUS_BPS` discount.\n    /// @param borrower Position to liquidate.\n    /// @param repayAmount Debt token the caller is willing to repay. Capped at the\n    ///        close factor, and further capped by the collateral actually available.\n    /// @param minCollateralOut Slippage bound; revert if less collateral is seized.\n    ///        Set this — the price can move between simulation and inclusion.\n    /// @return repaid Debt token actually pulled from the liquidator.\n    /// @return seized Collateral transferred to the liquidator.\n    /// @dev Not pausable. Liquidation is the mechanism that keeps the pool solvent;\n    ///      it must not be switchable off.\n    function liquidate(address borrower, uint256 repayAmount, address to, uint256 minCollateralOut)\n        external\n        nonReentrant\n        returns (uint256 repaid, uint256 seized)\n    {\n        if (repayAmount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        if (borrower == address(0) || to == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n\n        accrueInterest();\n\n        uint256 debt = _debtOf(borrower);\n        if (debt == 0) revert NoDebt();\n\n        (repaid, seized) = _computeLiquidation(borrower, debt, repayAmount);\n        if (seized < minCollateralOut) revert SlippageProtection(seized, minCollateralOut);\n\n        uint256 sharesBurned;\n        if (repaid == debt) {\n            sharesBurned = debtSharesOf[borrower];\n        } else {\n            sharesBurned = repaid.mulDiv(WAD, borrowIndex, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n            // A repay too small to burn a single share would take collateral\n            // without reducing debt. Reject it.\n            if (sharesBurned == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        }\n\n        // Effects.\n        debtSharesOf[borrower] -= sharesBurned;\n        totalDebtShares -= sharesBurned;\n        collateralOf[borrower] -= seized;\n\n        // Interactions.\n        if (_pull(debtToken, msg.sender, repaid) != repaid) revert SlippageProtection(0, repaid);\n        collateralToken.safeTransfer(to, seized);\n\n        emit Liquidated(msg.sender, borrower, repaid, seized, sharesBurned);\n    }\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Views\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    /// @notice Current debt of `account`, including interest accrued so far.\n    /// @dev View-only: does not write the index. Values reflect `block.timestamp`.\n    function debtOf(address account) public view returns (uint256) {\n        return debtSharesOf[account].mulDiv(_currentBorrowIndex(), WAD, Math.Rounding.Ceil);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Total outstanding borrows including pending interest.\n    function totalDebt() public view returns (uint256) {\n        return totalDebtShares.mulDiv(_currentBorrowIndex(), WAD, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Idle debt token held by the pool.\n    function availableLiquidity() external view returns (uint256) {\n        return _cash();\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Debt token backing all supply shares: idle cash plus outstanding debt.\n    function totalAssets() external view returns (uint256) {\n        return _cash() + totalDebt();\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Assets a lender's shares are currently worth.\n    function convertToAssets(uint256 shares) external view returns (uint256) {\n        return shares.mulDiv(_cash() + totalDebt() + VIRTUAL_ASSETS, totalSupplyShares + VIRTUAL_SHARES, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Shares that supplying `assets` would mint right now.\n    function convertToShares(uint256 assets) external view returns (uint256) {\n        return assets.mulDiv(totalSupplyShares + VIRTUAL_SHARES, _cash() + totalDebt() + VIRTUAL_ASSETS, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Full health snapshot of a position.\n    /// @return collateralValueUsd 1e18-scaled USD value of locked collateral.\n    /// @return debtValueUsd 1e18-scaled USD value of outstanding debt.\n    /// @return healthFactorWad 1e18 == exactly at the liquidation threshold; below\n    ///         1e18 means liquidatable. `type(uint256).max` when there is no debt.\n    /// @return liquidatable Whether {liquidate} would accept this position now.\n    function positionHealth(address account)\n        external\n        view\n        returns (uint256 collateralValueUsd, uint256 debtValueUsd, uint256 healthFactorWad, bool liquidatable)\n    {\n        collateralValueUsd = _collateralValueUsd(collateralOf[account], oracle.collateralPriceUsd());\n        debtValueUsd = _debtValueUsd(debtOf(account), oracle.debtPriceUsd());\n        healthFactorWad = _healthFactor(collateralValueUsd, debtValueUsd);\n        liquidatable = debtValueUsd * BPS > collateralValueUsd * LIQUIDATION_THRESHOLD_BPS;\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Additional debt token `account` could borrow right now, ignoring\n    ///         available liquidity.\n    function maxBorrowable(address account) external view returns (uint256) {\n        uint256 collateralValue = _collateralValueUsd(collateralOf[account], oracle.collateralPriceUsd());\n        uint256 debtPrice = oracle.debtPriceUsd();\n        uint256 debtValueAllowed = collateralValue.mulDiv(MAX_LTV_BPS, BPS, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n        uint256 debtValueNow = _debtValueUsd(debtOf(account), debtPrice);\n        if (debtValueNow >= debtValueAllowed) return 0;\n        return (debtValueAllowed - debtValueNow).mulDiv(_debtUnit, debtPrice, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Simulate a liquidation: what a given repay would seize, after the\n    ///         close-factor and collateral caps are applied.\n    /// @dev Reverts for the same reasons {liquidate} would, so a liquidator bot can\n    ///      use it as a dry run.\n    function previewLiquidation(address borrower, uint256 repayAmount)\n        external\n        view\n        returns (uint256 repaid, uint256 seized)\n    {\n        uint256 debt = debtOf(borrower);\n        if (debt == 0) revert NoDebt();\n        return _computeLiquidation(borrower, debt, repayAmount);\n    }\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Owner controls\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    /// @notice Point the pool at a different price source.\n    /// @dev The single most dangerous privilege here: a malicious oracle can make\n    ///      every position liquidatable. Owner MUST be a timelock behind a multisig.\n    function setOracle(address newOracle) external onlyOwner {\n        if (newOracle == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n        // Sanity-check the new source before committing to it.\n        if (IPriceOracle(newOracle).collateralPriceUsd() == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        if (IPriceOracle(newOracle).debtPriceUsd() == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n\n        emit OracleUpdated(address(oracle), newOracle);\n        oracle = IPriceOracle(newOracle);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Change the flat annual borrow rate.\n    /// @dev Accrues at the old rate first so the change is never retroactive.\n    function setInterestRate(uint256 newRateBps) external onlyOwner {\n        if (newRateBps > MAX_INTEREST_RATE_BPS) revert RateTooHigh(newRateBps);\n        accrueInterest();\n        emit InterestRateUpdated(interestRateBps, newRateBps);\n        interestRateBps = newRateBps;\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Change the dust floor on position size.\n    function setMinDebt(uint256 newMinDebt) external onlyOwner {\n        if (newMinDebt > maxMinDebt) revert MinDebtTooHigh(newMinDebt);\n        emit MinDebtUpdated(minDebt, newMinDebt);\n        minDebt = newMinDebt;\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Halt new supply and new borrows.\n    /// @dev Repay, withdraw, and liquidate stay open while paused by design — the\n    ///      owner can stop the pool growing, but cannot trap anyone's funds.\n    function pause() external onlyOwner {\n        _pause();\n    }\n\n    function unpause() external onlyOwner {\n        _unpause();\n    }\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Internals\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    function _cash() private view returns (uint256) {\n        return debtToken.balanceOf(address(this));\n    }\n\n    function _totalAssets() private view returns (uint256) {\n        return _cash() + totalDebtShares.mulDiv(borrowIndex, WAD, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n    }\n\n    /// @dev Index including interest that has accrued but not yet been written.\n    function _currentBorrowIndex() private view returns (uint256) {\n        uint256 elapsed = block.timestamp - lastAccrualTimestamp;\n        if (elapsed == 0 || totalDebtShares == 0) return borrowIndex;\n        return borrowIndex + borrowIndex.mulDiv(interestRateBps * elapsed, BPS * SECONDS_PER_YEAR);\n    }\n\n    /// @dev Only valid immediately after {accrueInterest}.\n    function _debtOf(address account) private view returns (uint256) {\n        return debtSharesOf[account].mulDiv(borrowIndex, WAD, Math.Rounding.Ceil);\n    }\n\n    function _collateralValueUsd(uint256 amount, uint256 price) private view returns (uint256) {\n        return amount.mulDiv(price, _collateralUnit, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n    }\n\n    function _debtValueUsd(uint256 amount, uint256 price) private view returns (uint256) {\n        return amount.mulDiv(price, _debtUnit, Math.Rounding.Ceil);\n    }\n\n    function _healthFactor(uint256 collateralValue, uint256 debtValue) private pure returns (uint256) {\n        if (debtValue == 0) return type(uint256).max;\n        return collateralValue.mulDiv(LIQUIDATION_THRESHOLD_BPS * WAD, debtValue * BPS, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n    }\n\n    /// @dev Shared by {liquidate} and {previewLiquidation} so the simulation and the\n    ///      real thing can never drift apart. Reverts if the position is not\n    ///      liquidatable or the requested repay breaches the close factor.\n    /// @param debt Borrower debt at the caller's chosen index (written or pending).\n    function _computeLiquidation(address borrower, uint256 debt, uint256 repayAmount)\n        private\n        view\n        returns (uint256 repaid, uint256 seized)\n    {\n        uint256 collateralPrice = oracle.collateralPriceUsd();\n        uint256 debtPrice = oracle.debtPriceUsd();\n\n        uint256 collateral = collateralOf[borrower];\n        uint256 collateralValue = _collateralValueUsd(collateral, collateralPrice);\n        uint256 debtValue = _debtValueUsd(debt, debtPrice);\n\n        // Liquidatable iff debt value exceeds the liquidation threshold.\n        if (debtValue * BPS <= collateralValue * LIQUIDATION_THRESHOLD_BPS) {\n            revert PositionHealthy(_healthFactor(collateralValue, debtValue));\n        }\n\n        uint256 maxRepay = debt.mulDiv(MAX_CLOSE_FACTOR_BPS, BPS, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n        // Never leave a dust remainder nobody will bother to liquidate.\n        if (debt - maxRepay < minDebt) maxRepay = debt;\n        if (repayAmount > maxRepay) revert RepayExceedsCloseFactor(repayAmount, maxRepay);\n\n        repaid = repayAmount;\n        seized = _collateralForRepay(repaid, collateralPrice, debtPrice);\n\n        if (seized > collateral) {\n            // Underwater beyond the bonus: the liquidator takes all remaining\n            // collateral and only pays what that collateral is worth net of the\n            // bonus. The shortfall stays with the pool as bad debt rather than\n            // being silently charged to the liquidator.\n            seized = collateral;\n            repaid = _repayForCollateral(seized, collateralPrice, debtPrice);\n            if (repaid > debt) repaid = debt;\n        }\n\n        if (seized == 0 || repaid == 0) revert NothingSeized();\n    }\n\n    /// @dev Collateral owed for repaying `repayAmount`, including the bonus.\n    ///      Rounds down — the liquidator never receives more than the formula.\n    function _collateralForRepay(uint256 repayAmount, uint256 collateralPrice, uint256 debtPrice)\n        private\n        view\n        returns (uint256)\n    {\n        uint256 repayValue = repayAmount.mulDiv(debtPrice, _debtUnit, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n        uint256 seizeValue = repayValue.mulDiv(BPS + LIQUIDATION_BONUS_BPS, BPS, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n        return seizeValue.mulDiv(_collateralUnit, collateralPrice, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n    }\n\n    /// @dev Inverse of {_collateralForRepay}: the repay that exactly buys `seized`.\n    ///      Rounds down so a clamped liquidator is never overcharged.\n    function _repayForCollateral(uint256 seized, uint256 collateralPrice, uint256 debtPrice)\n        private\n        view\n        returns (uint256)\n    {\n        uint256 seizeValue = seized.mulDiv(collateralPrice, _collateralUnit, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n        uint256 repayValue = seizeValue.mulDiv(BPS, BPS + LIQUIDATION_BONUS_BPS, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n        return repayValue.mulDiv(_debtUnit, debtPrice, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n    }\n\n    /// @dev Reverts unless `account` is at or below {MAX_LTV_BPS}. Debt-free\n    ///      positions short-circuit without touching the oracle.\n    function _requireWithinMaxLtv(address account) private view {\n        uint256 debt = _debtOf(account);\n        if (debt == 0) return;\n\n        uint256 collateralValue = _collateralValueUsd(collateralOf[account], oracle.collateralPriceUsd());\n        uint256 debtValue = _debtValueUsd(debt, oracle.debtPriceUsd());\n\n        if (debtValue * BPS > collateralValue * MAX_LTV_BPS) {\n            revert PositionUnhealthy(_healthFactor(collateralValue, debtValue));\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// @dev Pulls tokens and returns the amount actually received, so a\n    ///      fee-on-transfer token can never credit more than it delivered.\n    function _pull(IERC20 token, address from, uint256 amount) private returns (uint256) {\n        uint256 balanceBefore = token.balanceOf(address(this));\n        token.safeTransferFrom(from, address(this), amount);\n        return token.balanceOf(address(this)) - balanceBefore;\n    }\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":440,"oldLines":6,"newStart":440,"newLines":30,"lines":["         emit Liquidated(msg.sender, borrower, repaid, seized, sharesBurned);","     }"," ","+    /// @notice Erase the debt of a position that has no collateral left.","+    /// @dev Permissionless and moves no tokens. A position can only reach","+    ///      \"zero collateral, non-zero debt\" by being liquidated while deeply","+    ///      underwater, so the remainder is genuinely unbacked. Recognising it","+    ///      immediately drops the pool's share price for everyone at once. If it","+    ///      lingered, `totalAssets` would keep counting uncollectable debt as an","+    ///      asset and the lenders who withdrew first would be paid out of the","+    ///      lenders who withdrew last.","+    /// @return written Debt token amount written off.","+    function writeOffBadDebt(address borrower) external nonReentrant returns (uint256 written) {","+        accrueInterest();","+","+        if (collateralOf[borrower] != 0) revert PositionHasCollateral();","+","+        uint256 shares = debtSharesOf[borrower];","+        if (shares == 0) revert NoDebt();","+","+        written = _debtOf(borrower);","+        debtSharesOf[borrower] = 0;","+        totalDebtShares -= shares;","+","+        emit BadDebtWrittenOff(borrower, written, shares);","+    }","+","     // ---------------------------------------------------------------------","     // Views","     // ---------------------------------------------------------------------"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}

   function test_liquidationSlippageBoundEnforced() public {","newString":"    function test_badDebtWriteOffDropsSharePriceForEveryone() public {\n        _openPosition(10 ether, 14_000e6);\n        ethFeed.set(700e8, block.timestamp);\n\n        vm.prank(keeper);\n        pool.liquidate(alice, 7_000e6, keeper, 0);\n\n        uint256 shares = pool.supplySharesOf(lender);\n        uint256 before = pool.convertToAssets(shares);\n\n        // Position is collateral-free with debt left over: pure bad debt.\n        assertEq(pool.collateralOf(alice), 0);\n        uint256 written = pool.writeOffBadDebt(alice);\n\n        assertGt(written, 0);\n        assertEq(pool.debtOf(alice), 0);\n        // Lenders take the loss immediately rather than on a first-come basis.\n        assertLt(pool.convertToAssets(shares), before);\n    }\n\n    function test_writeOffRequiresNoCollateral() public {\n        _openPosition(10 ether, 14_000e6);\n        vm.expectRevert(LendingPool.PositionHasCollateral.selector);\n        pool.writeOffBadDebt(alice);\n    }\n\n    function test_liquidationSlippageBoundEnforced() public {","originalFile":"// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity 0.8.28;\n\nimport {Test} from \"forge-std/Test.sol\";\n\nimport {ChainlinkPriceOracle} from \"../src/ChainlinkPriceOracle.sol\";\nimport {IAggregatorV3} from \"../src/interfaces/IAggregatorV3.sol\";\nimport {LendingPool} from \"../src/LendingPool.sol\";\nimport {MockAggregator, MockERC20} from \"./mocks/Mocks.sol\";\n\ncontract LendingPoolTest is Test {\n    MockERC20 internal weth;\n    MockERC20 internal usdc;\n    MockAggregator internal ethFeed;\n    MockAggregator internal usdcFeed;\n    ChainlinkPriceOracle internal oracle;\n    LendingPool internal pool;\n\n    address internal owner = makeAddr(\"owner\");\n    address internal lender = makeAddr(\"lender\");\n    address internal alice = makeAddr(\"alice\");\n    address internal keeper = makeAddr(\"keeper\");\n\n    uint256 internal constant RATE_BPS = 500; // 5% APR\n    uint256 internal constant MIN_DEBT = 100e6; // 100 USDC\n\n    function setUp() public {\n        vm.warp(1_700_000_000);\n\n        weth = new MockERC20(\"Wrapped Ether\", \"WETH\", 18);\n        usdc = new MockERC20(\"USD Coin\", \"USDC\", 6);\n\n        ethFeed = new MockAggregator(8, 2000e8);\n        usdcFeed = new MockAggregator(8, 1e8);\n\n        oracle = new ChainlinkPriceOracle(\n            ChainlinkPriceOracle.FeedConfig({\n                feed: IAggregatorV3(address(ethFeed)), maxStaleness: 1 hours, minAnswer: 100e8, maxAnswer: 1_000_000e8\n            }),\n            ChainlinkPriceOracle.FeedConfig({\n                feed: IAggregatorV3(address(usdcFeed)), maxStaleness: 24 hours, minAnswer: 0.5e8, maxAnswer: 2e8\n            })\n        );\n\n        pool = new LendingPool(address(weth), address(usdc), address(oracle), owner, RATE_BPS, MIN_DEBT);\n\n        usdc.mint(lender, 1_000_000e6);\n        usdc.mint(keeper, 1_000_000e6);\n        usdc.mint(alice, 1_000_000e6);\n        weth.mint(alice, 1_000 ether);\n\n        vm.prank(lender);\n        usdc.approve(address(pool), type(uint256).max);\n        vm.prank(keeper);\n        usdc.approve(address(pool), type(uint256).max);\n        vm.startPrank(alice);\n        usdc.approve(address(pool), type(uint256).max);\n        weth.approve(address(pool), type(uint256).max);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n\n        vm.prank(lender);\n        pool.supply(500_000e6, lender, 0);\n    }\n\n    function _openPosition(uint256 collateral, uint256 borrowAmount) internal {\n        vm.startPrank(alice);\n        pool.depositCollateral(collateral, alice);\n        pool.borrow(borrowAmount, alice);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n    }\n\n    // -----------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Borrowing limits\n    // -----------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    function test_borrowUpToMaxLtv() public {\n        // 10 WETH @ $2000 = $20,000 collateral -> 70% = 14,000 USDC.\n        _openPosition(10 ether, 14_000e6);\n\n        assertEq(pool.debtOf(alice), 14_000e6);\n        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(alice), 1_000_000e6 + 14_000e6);\n        assertEq(pool.maxBorrowable(alice), 0);\n    }\n\n    function test_borrowAboveMaxLtvReverts() public {\n        vm.startPrank(alice);\n        pool.depositCollateral(10 ether, alice);\n        vm.expectRevert();\n        pool.borrow(14_000e6 + 1, alice);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n    }\n\n    function test_borrowBelowMinDebtReverts() public {\n        vm.startPrank(alice);\n        pool.depositCollateral(10 ether, alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(abi.encodeWithSelector(LendingPool.DebtBelowMinimum.selector, 1e6, MIN_DEBT));\n        pool.borrow(1e6, alice);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n    }\n\n    function test_withdrawCollateralBlockedWhileUnderwaterOfMaxLtv() public {\n        _openPosition(10 ether, 14_000e6);\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert();\n        pool.withdrawCollateral(1, alice);\n    }\n\n    function test_withdrawCollateralAllowedWhenHealthy() public {\n        _openPosition(10 ether, 7_000e6);\n        // $7,000 debt needs $10,000 collateral at 70% LTV = 5 WETH.\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        pool.withdrawCollateral(5 ether, alice);\n        assertEq(pool.collateralOf(alice), 5 ether);\n    }\n\n    function test_debtFreeWithdrawWorksWithDeadOracle() public {\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        pool.depositCollateral(1 ether, alice);\n\n        // Oracle now reverts on every read.\n        vm.warp(block.timestamp + 30 days);\n\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        pool.withdrawCollateral(1 ether, alice);\n        assertEq(weth.balanceOf(alice), 1_000 ether);\n    }\n\n    // -----------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Interest\n    // -----------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    function test_interestAccrues() public {\n        _openPosition(10 ether, 10_000e6);\n        assertEq(pool.debtOf(alice), 10_000e6);\n\n        vm.warp(block.timestamp + 365 days);\n        // 5% flat annual on 10,000 USDC.\n        assertApproxEqAbs(pool.debtOf(alice), 10_500e6, 1);\n\n        // Lenders capture it: 500 USDC of interest across 500,000 supplied.\n        uint256 lenderAssets = pool.convertToAssets(pool.supplySharesOf(lender));\n        assertApproxEqAbs(lenderAssets, 500_500e6, 2);\n    }\n\n    function test_repayFullClearsDebt() public {\n        _openPosition(10 ether, 10_000e6);\n        vm.warp(block.timestamp + 180 days);\n\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        uint256 repaid = pool.repay(type(uint256).max, alice);\n\n        assertGt(repaid, 10_000e6);\n        assertEq(pool.debtOf(alice), 0);\n        assertEq(pool.debtSharesOf(alice), 0);\n        assertEq(pool.totalDebtShares(), 0);\n    }\n\n    function test_noInterestWhenNoBorrowers() public {\n        uint256 indexBefore = pool.borrowIndex();\n        vm.warp(block.timestamp + 365 days);\n        pool.accrueInterest();\n        assertEq(pool.borrowIndex(), indexBefore);\n    }\n\n    // -----------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Liquidation\n    // -----------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    function test_liquidateSeizesCollateralPlusBonus() public {\n        _openPosition(10 ether, 14_000e6);\n\n        // ETH -> $1,600. Collateral $16,000, debt $14,000 = 87.5% > 85%.\n        ethFeed.set(1600e8, block.timestamp);\n\n        (,, uint256 hf, bool liquidatable) = pool.positionHealth(alice);\n        assertTrue(liquidatable);\n        assertLt(hf, 1e18);\n\n        uint256 repayAmount = 7_000e6; // exactly the close factor\n        (uint256 previewRepaid, uint256 previewSeized) = pool.previewLiquidation(alice, repayAmount);\n\n        vm.prank(keeper);\n        (uint256 repaid, uint256 seized) = pool.liquidate(alice, repayAmount, keeper, previewSeized);\n\n        assertEq(repaid, previewRepaid);\n        assertEq(seized, previewSeized);\n        // $7,000 * 1.05 / $1,600 = 4.59375 WETH.\n        assertEq(seized, 4.59375 ether);\n        assertEq(weth.balanceOf(keeper), seized);\n        assertApproxEqAbs(pool.debtOf(alice), 7_000e6, 1);\n\n        // Position is healthy again.\n        (,,, bool stillLiquidatable) = pool.positionHealth(alice);\n        assertFalse(stillLiquidatable);\n    }\n\n    function test_liquidateHealthyPositionReverts() public {\n        _openPosition(10 ether, 14_000e6);\n        vm.prank(keeper);\n        vm.expectRevert();\n        pool.liquidate(alice, 1_000e6, keeper, 0);\n    }\n\n    function test_liquidateAboveCloseFactorReverts() public {\n        _openPosition(10 ether, 14_000e6);\n        ethFeed.set(1600e8, block.timestamp);\n\n        vm.prank(keeper);\n        vm.expectRevert(abi.encodeWithSelector(LendingPool.RepayExceedsCloseFactor.selector, 7_000e6 + 1, 7_000e6));\n        pool.liquidate(alice, 7_000e6 + 1, keeper, 0);\n    }\n\n    function test_liquidateClampsWhenUnderwater() public {\n        _openPosition(10 ether, 14_000e6);\n\n        // ETH collapses to $700: collateral $7,000 against $14,000 of debt. Even a\n        // half-close cannot be paid for out of the remaining collateral.\n        ethFeed.set(700e8, block.timestamp);\n\n        vm.prank(keeper);\n        (uint256 repaid, uint256 seized) = pool.liquidate(alice, 7_000e6, keeper, 0);\n\n        // All collateral is taken; the liquidator only pays what it is worth net\n        // of the 5% bonus: $7,000 / 1.05 = 6,666.666666 USDC.\n        assertEq(seized, 10 ether);\n        assertEq(repaid, 6_666_666666);\n        assertEq(pool.collateralOf(alice), 0);\n        // The remainder is bad debt sitting on the pool.\n        assertGt(pool.debtOf(alice), 0);\n    }\n\n    function test_liquidationSlippageBoundEnforced() public {\n        _openPosition(10 ether, 14_000e6);\n        ethFeed.set(1600e8, block.timestamp);\n\n        vm.prank(keeper);\n        vm.expectRevert();\n        pool.liquidate(alice, 7_000e6, keeper, 5 ether);\n    }\n\n    // -----------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Oracle safety\n    // -----------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    function test_stalePriceBlocksBorrow() public {\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        pool.depositCollateral(10 ether, alice);\n\n        vm.warp(block.timestamp + 2 hours); // ETH feed heartbeat is 1 hour\n\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(\n            abi.encodeWithSelector(\n                ChainlinkPriceOracle.StalePrice.selector, address(ethFeed), block.timestamp - 2 hours\n            )\n        );\n        pool.borrow(1_000e6, alice);\n    }\n\n    function test_priceOutOfBoundsBlocksBorrow() public {\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        pool.depositCollateral(10 ether, alice);\n\n        ethFeed.set(50e8, block.timestamp); // below the configured floor\n\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(\n            abi.encodeWithSelector(ChainlinkPriceOracle.PriceOutOfBounds.selector, address(ethFeed), int256(50e8))\n        );\n        pool.borrow(1_000e6, alice);\n    }\n\n    function test_debtTokenDepegRaisesDebtValue() public {\n        _openPosition(10 ether, 14_000e6);\n        assertFalse(_liquidatable(alice));\n\n        // USDC trades at $1.25: the debt is now worth $17,500 against $20,000 of\n        // collateral, i.e. 87.5% — past the 85% threshold.\n        usdcFeed.set(1.25e8, block.timestamp);\n        assertTrue(_liquidatable(alice));\n    }\n\n    // -----------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Lender share accounting\n    // -----------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    function test_donationDoesNotStealFromNextDepositor() public {\n        LendingPool fresh = new LendingPool(address(weth), address(usdc), address(oracle), owner, RATE_BPS, MIN_DEBT);\n\n        address attacker = makeAddr(\"attacker\");\n        usdc.mint(attacker, 1_000_000e6);\n        vm.startPrank(attacker);\n        usdc.approve(address(fresh), type(uint256).max);\n        fresh.supply(1, attacker, 0);\n        usdc.transfer(address(fresh), 100_000e6); // direct donation\n        vm.stopPrank();\n\n        vm.prank(lender);\n        usdc.approve(address(fresh), type(uint256).max);\n        vm.prank(lender);\n        uint256 shares = fresh.supply(10_000e6, lender, 0);\n\n        assertGt(shares, 0);\n        // The victim can still redeem approximately what they put in.\n        assertGe(fresh.convertToAssets(shares), 9_999e6);\n    }\n\n    function test_withdrawBlockedByUtilisationNotByPause() public {\n        _openPosition(300 ether, 400_000e6);\n\n        vm.prank(owner);\n        pool.pause();\n\n        // Pausing does not trap lenders...\n        uint256 shares = pool.supplySharesOf(lender);\n        vm.prank(lender);\n        uint256 assets = pool.withdraw(shares / 10, lender, 0);\n        assertGt(assets, 0);\n\n        // ...but absent liquidity does.\n        uint256 remaining = pool.supplySharesOf(lender);\n        vm.prank(lender);\n        vm.expectRevert(LendingPool.InsufficientLiquidity.selector);\n        pool.withdraw(remaining, lender, 0);\n    }\n\n    function test_pauseStopsBorrowAndSupplyOnly() public {\n        _openPosition(10 ether, 10_000e6);\n\n        vm.prank(owner);\n        pool.pause();\n\n        vm.startPrank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert();\n        pool.borrow(1e6, alice);\n\n        // Repay and collateral withdrawal still work.\n        pool.repay(type(uint256).max, alice);\n        pool.withdrawCollateral(10 ether, alice);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n    }\n\n    // -----------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Access control\n    // -----------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    function test_onlyOwnerControls() public {\n        vm.startPrank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert();\n        pool.setInterestRate(100);\n        vm.expectRevert();\n        pool.setOracle(address(oracle));\n        vm.expectRevert();\n        pool.setMinDebt(0);\n        vm.expectRevert();\n        pool.pause();\n        vm.stopPrank();\n    }\n\n    function test_interestRateIsCapped() public {\n        vm.prank(owner);\n        vm.expectRevert(abi.encodeWithSelector(LendingPool.RateTooHigh.selector, 5_001));\n        pool.setInterestRate(5_001);\n    }\n\n    // -----------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Fuzz\n    // -----------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    /// @dev Whatever the collateral, a fresh borrow at exactly max LTV must succeed\n    ///      and one wei more must fail.\n    function testFuzz_maxLtvBoundary(uint256 collateral) public {\n        collateral = bound(collateral, 0.5 ether, 300 ether);\n\n        vm.startPrank(alice);\n        pool.depositCollateral(collateral, alice);\n        uint256 max = pool.maxBorrowable(alice);\n        vm.assume(max >= MIN_DEBT);\n\n        vm.expectRevert();\n        pool.borrow(max + 1e6, alice);\n\n        pool.borrow(max, alice);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n\n        assertFalse(_liquidatable(alice));\n    }\n\n    /// @dev A liquidator never gets more collateral than the bonus formula allows.\n    function testFuzz_liquidationBonusIsBounded(uint256 repayAmount) public {\n        _openPosition(10 ether, 14_000e6);\n        ethFeed.set(1600e8, block.timestamp);\n\n        repayAmount = bound(repayAmount, 1e6, 7_000e6);\n\n        vm.prank(keeper);\n        (uint256 repaid, uint256 seized) = pool.liquidate(alice, repayAmount, keeper, 0);\n\n        uint256 seizedValue = (seized * 1600e18) / 1e18; // USD, 1e18-scaled\n        uint256 repaidValue = repaid * 1e12 * 1e18 / 1e18; // USDC at $1, 1e18-scaled\n        assertLe(seizedValue, (repaidValue * 10_500) / 10_000);\n    }\n\n    function _liquidatable(address account) internal view returns (bool l) {\n        (,,, l) = pool.positionHealth(account);\n    }\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":229,"oldLines":6,"newStart":229,"newLines":32,"lines":["         assertGt(pool.debtOf(alice), 0);","     }"," ","+    function test_badDebtWriteOffDropsSharePriceForEveryone() public {","+        _openPosition(10 ether, 14_000e6);","+        ethFeed.set(700e8, block.timestamp);","+","+        vm.prank(keeper);","+        pool.liquidate(alice, 7_000e6, keeper, 0);","+","+        uint256 shares = pool.supplySharesOf(lender);","+        uint256 before = pool.convertToAssets(shares);","+","+        // Position is collateral-free with debt left over: pure bad debt.","+        assertEq(pool.collateralOf(alice), 0);","+        uint256 written = pool.writeOffBadDebt(alice);","+","+        assertGt(written, 0);","+        assertEq(pool.debtOf(alice), 0);","+        // Lenders take the loss immediately rather than on a first-come basis.","+        assertLt(pool.convertToAssets(shares), before);","+    }","+","+    function test_writeOffRequiresNoCollateral() public {","+        _openPosition(10 ether, 14_000e6);","+        vm.expectRevert(LendingPool.PositionHasCollateral.selector);","+        pool.writeOffBadDebt(alice);","+    }","+","     function test_liquidationSlippageBoundEnforced() public {","         _openPosition(10 ether, 14_000e6);","         ethFeed.set(1600e8, block.timestamp);"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false,"staleRecovered":true}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CdUU9UqDNoMDm3KWRVPDA","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_017iAGv5wLA7Abt1Kqooy4Bg","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/security-goal-002/2026-07-28T122808Z-claude-with-skill-1/workspace/NOTES.md","content":"# WETH/USDC borrowing market — operator & integrator notes\n\nTwo contracts get deployed:\n\n| Contract | Role |\n|---|---|\n| `src/ChainlinkPriceOracle.sol` | Immutable adapter over two Chainlink USD feeds. Returns 1e18-scaled prices, or reverts. |\n| `src/LendingPool.sol` | The market. Holds collateral, holds lender liquidity, tracks debt, runs liquidations. |\n\nLenders supply USDC and hold supply shares; borrowers lock WETH and draw USDC\nagainst it. `forge build` compiles; `forge test` runs 26 tests including two\nfuzz properties.\n\nParameters are fixed constants, not governance knobs: max LTV 70%, liquidation\nthreshold 85%, liquidation bonus 5%, close factor 50%. Changing them means\ndeploying a new pool.\n\n---\n\n## 1. How a position's health is computed\n\nEverything is compared in **1e18-scaled USD**, never in token units, because\nWETH has 18 decimals and USDC has 6. Both decimals are read from the tokens at\nconstruction (`_collateralUnit`, `_debtUnit`) — nothing is hardcoded.\n\n```\ncollateralValue = collateralAmount * collateralPriceUsd / 1e18   (WETH units)\ndebtValue       = debtAmount       * debtPriceUsd       / 1e6    (USDC units)\n```\n\nNote that **both legs are priced**. The debt is not assumed to be worth exactly\n$1. If USDC trades at $1.25, a position's debt value rises 25% and it can become\nliquidatable on a stablecoin move alone — which is correct, and is what a market\nthat hardcoded `1.0` would miss.\n\nTwo different thresholds apply:\n\n| Check | Rule | Where |\n|---|---|---|\n| **Max LTV (70%)** | `debtValue * 10000 <= collateralValue * 7000` | Enforced after `borrow` and after `withdrawCollateral` |\n| **Liquidation threshold (85%)** | `debtValue * 10000 > collateralValue * 8500` → liquidatable | Enforced in `liquidate` |\n\nThe gap between 70% and 85% is the borrower's buffer. A fresh borrow can only\nreach 70%; the position becomes liquidatable once price movement or accrued\ninterest pushes it past 85%.\n\n`positionHealth(account)` returns both USD values plus a **health factor**:\n\n```\nhealthFactor = collateralValue * 8500 * 1e18 / (debtValue * 10000)\n```\n\n`1e18` means exactly at the liquidation threshold; below `1e18` means\nliquidatable; `type(uint256).max` means no debt.\n\n### Debt and interest\n\nDebt is stored as **index shares**, not absolute amounts. A single global\n`borrowIndex` (starts at `1e18`) grows with every `accrueInterest()`:\n\n```\ngrowth      = borrowIndex * rateBps * elapsedSeconds / (10000 * 365 days)\nborrowIndex = borrowIndex + growth\ndebtOf(u)   = debtShares[u] * borrowIndex / 1e18     (rounded up)\n```\n\nSimple interest within each window, compounding whenever anyone touches the\npool. Every state-changing entry point calls `accrueInterest()` first, so no\naction ever runs on a stale index. With no borrowers the index does not move —\nthe timestamp still advances, so an idle period never gets charged retroactively.\n\nRounding is consistently in the pool's favour: debt owed rounds **up**,\ncollateral seized and lender assets paid out round **down**.\n\n### Available liquidity ≠ health\n\nA healthy position can still fail to borrow if lenders' USDC is already lent\nout (`InsufficientLiquidity`). That is a utilisation limit, not a health limit.\n\n---\n\n## 2. What a liquidator has to do\n\n**Preconditions.** `debtValue * 10000 > collateralValue * 8500`. Check with\n`positionHealth(borrower)` (`liquidatable == true`) and dry-run the exact\nnumbers with `previewLiquidation(borrower, repayAmount)` — it runs the identical\ncode path as the real call and reverts for the same reasons.\n\n**The call.**\n\n```solidity\nusdc.approve(address(pool), repayAmount);\npool.liquidate(borrower, repayAmount, recipient, minCollateralOut);\n```\n\n- `repayAmount` — USDC you are willing to repay. Capped at the **50% close\n  factor**. Exception: if closing 50% would leave less than `minDebt` behind,\n  the cap becomes 100% so no unliquidatable dust position is created.\n- `minCollateralOut` — **set this.** It is your slippage bound. The price can\n  move between your simulation and your inclusion; without a bound you can be\n  filled at materially less WETH than you priced.\n\n**What you get.**\n\n```\nseizeValueUsd = repayAmount * debtPriceUsd / 1e6 * 10500 / 10000\nseizedWeth    = seizeValueUsd * 1e18 / collateralPriceUsd\n```\n\ni.e. the USD you repaid plus a **5% bonus**, paid in WETH, rounded down.\n\n**Underwater positions.** If the computed seize exceeds the collateral the\nborrower actually has (price fell more than the 5% bonus covers), the pool\nclamps: you take *all* remaining collateral and are only charged what that\ncollateral is worth net of the bonus. You are never overcharged for a position\nthat cannot pay the bonus. The unpaid remainder becomes bad debt on the pool.\n\n**Bad debt cleanup.** After such a liquidation the borrower has zero collateral\nand residual debt. Anyone can call `writeOffBadDebt(borrower)` — permissionless,\nmoves no tokens — which burns those debt shares. Until it is called, the pool\ncounts uncollectable debt as an asset, so lender share price is overstated and\nwhoever withdraws first is paid out of whoever withdraws last. A keeper should\ncall it in the same bundle as the liquidation.\n\n**Practical notes.**\n\n- Liquidation is **not pausable** and has no allowlist. Anyone, any time.\n- It reverts if the oracle is stale or out of bounds. During a feed outage\n  nobody can liquidate — see the operator risks below.\n- Liquidations are MEV-competitive. Route through a private mempool\n  (Flashbots Protect / MEV-Share) or your transaction is a free option for\n  anyone watching, who will copy it and frontrun you.\n- The seized WETH is transferred, not swapped. The pool never touches a DEX,\n  so it carries no swap or sandwich risk of its own; unwinding the WETH is your\n  problem and your slippage.\n\n---\n\n## 3. What an operator has to get right for mainnet\n\n### Before deploying\n\n**Verify every address yourself.** `script/Deploy.s.sol` hardcodes:\n\n| | Address |\n|---|---|\n| WETH | `0xC02aaA39b223FE8D0A0e5C4F27eAD9083C756Cc2` |\n| USDC | `0xA0b86991c6218b36c1d19D4a2e9Eb0cE3606eB48` |\n| ETH/USD feed | `0x5f4eC3Df9cbd43714FE2740f5E3616155c5b8419` (8 dec, ~1h heartbeat) |\n| USDC/USD feed | `0x8fFfFfd4AfB6115b954Bd326cbe7B4BA576818f6` (8 dec, ~24h heartbeat) |\n\nCheck them against `data.chain.link` and the token's own docs before broadcast —\ndo not take them from this file on trust. The script refuses to run off chain\nid 1.\n\n**Set `maxStaleness` per feed from the feed's actual heartbeat**, plus margin.\nThe ETH/USD and USDC/USD feeds have very different heartbeats (~1h vs ~24h);\nusing one number for both either makes the pool brittle or makes it accept a\nday-old ETH price. Constructor rejects anything above 24 hours.\n\n**Set the min/max answer band deliberately.** Chainlink aggregators clamp their\nanswer at the underlying aggregator's `minAnswer`/`maxAnswer`. If the real price\nleaves that band, the feed keeps reporting the boundary value and looks\nperfectly fresh — this is exactly how Venus lost money on LUNA. The bands in\nthis contract turn that silent lie into a revert. Set them wide enough to never\ntrip in normal markets, narrow enough to catch a broken feed.\n\n**Owner must be a timelock behind a multisig — never a deployer EOA.**\n`setOracle` is the single most dangerous privilege in the system: a malicious or\nmistaken oracle makes every position liquidatable at a price of the attacker's\nchoosing. `setInterestRate` and `setMinDebt` are bounded (50% APR, 10k USDC) but\n`setOracle` is not bounded by anything except the timelock delay.\n\n**Seed the pool before opening it.** The virtual-share offset (`1e6`) already\nmakes the first-depositor inflation attack uneconomical, but supplying a real\nfirst deposit from a controlled address and leaving it in costs nothing and\nremoves the question entirely.\n\n**`minDebt`.** Set it high enough that liquidating the smallest allowed position\nis profitable after mainnet gas. 100 USDC is a reasonable floor at current gas;\nat 100 gwei a ~250k-gas liquidation costs more than the 5% bonus on a small\nposition, and unprofitable positions never get liquidated. Revisit this if gas\nregimes change — it is adjustable up to 10,000 USDC.\n\n### Operating\n\n**Run liquidation keepers, or accept bad debt.** Nothing in the protocol\nliquidates anything. The 5% bonus is the entire incentive. Do not assume third\nparties will show up on day one for a small market — run your own keeper and\nwatch `positionHealth` for every open position. Also monitor for\ncollateral-free positions and call `writeOffBadDebt`.\n\n**Watch utilisation.** There is no interest rate model — the rate is flat and\ndoes not rise with utilisation. Nothing pushes utilisation back down when\nlenders want out. At 100% utilisation lenders simply cannot withdraw until\nsomeone repays. This is the most likely source of angry users. Manage it by\nsetting the rate manually (`setInterestRate`, which accrues at the old rate\nfirst, so changes are never retroactive) or by capping supply socially.\n\n**Pause semantics.** `pause()` stops **new supply and new borrows only**.\n`repay`, `withdraw`, `withdrawCollateral`, `liquidate` and `writeOffBadDebt`\nstay live while paused, by design: the owner can stop the pool from growing but\ncannot trap anyone's funds. That is deliberate — a pause switch that freezes\nuser exits is a censorship and rug vector, not a safety feature. Understand that\nthis means a pause does **not** stop an in-progress exploit that only uses exit\npaths.\n\n**Oracle outage is a liveness cliff.** If either feed goes stale or out of\nbounds, every priced operation reverts: no borrowing, no collateral withdrawal\nagainst debt, **and no liquidations**. Prices can keep moving while the pool is\nblind, and the pool accrues bad debt it cannot act on. Mitigations: generous\nstaleness margins, monitoring on feed freshness, and a rehearsed timelock\nproposal to swap in a replacement oracle. Debt-free depositors can always\nwithdraw their collateral — that path skips the oracle entirely.\n\n**USDC is pausable and has a blocklist.** If Circle pauses USDC or blocks an\naddress, repayments and liquidations involving it revert. There is nothing the\npool can do about this; know that it is a dependency.\n\n**Verify the source** on Etherscan immediately after deploy\n(`forge verify-contract`). Both contracts.\n\n### Known limitations — decide if you accept them\n\n- **No interest rate model.** Flat rate, owner-adjusted. Utilisation is\n  unmanaged (see above).\n- **No reserve factor.** 100% of interest goes to lenders; the protocol earns\n  nothing and has no reserve to absorb bad debt.\n- **Bad debt is socialised across lenders** via `writeOffBadDebt`. There is no\n  insurance fund.\n- **Single collateral, single debt asset, no isolation tiers.** One pool, one\n  pair.\n- **Not upgradeable.** No proxy, no `delegatecall` anywhere. Fixing anything\n  means a new deployment and a migration.\n- **No supply or borrow caps.** The market's size is unbounded.\n\n### Pre-deploy checks not done here\n\n- Run `slither .` and resolve findings (not installed in this environment).\n- Fork tests against real mainnet WETH/USDC/Chainlink, not just the mocks in\n  `test/mocks/`.\n- Independent audit. The tests here cover the paths I reasoned about; they are\n  not a substitute for someone trying to break it.\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":135,"cache_read_input_tokens":100516,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":135},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"ed135b88-1ee7-4ebc-b785-ba2b7d707846","uuid":"c3fc471f-1668-46f3-9b97-2a3f8102f850","timestamp":"2026-07-28T12:39:34.913Z","request_id":"req_011CdUU9TT82BYuxoPSBuhaf"}
